[Security Architecture](#11-security-architecture)\n12. [Scalability Patterns](#12-scalability-patterns)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Event-Driven Architecture\n\n### 1.1 Redis Stream Topology\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                          REDIS STREAMS TOPOLOGY                              │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  STREAMS (Append-only logs)                                          │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  osiris:raw:{source}     → Données brutes des seeders                │   │\n│  │  osiris:enriched         → Données enrichies (geo, IOC, metadata)    │   │\n│  │  osiris:intel            → Événements scorés + corrélés              │   │\n│  │  osiris:alerts           → Alertes critiques uniquement              │   │\n│  │  osiris:insights         → Insights IA (agent_insight)               │   │\n│  │  osiris:graph            → Événements pour mise à jour du graphe     │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  CONSUMER GROUPS (Traitement parallèle)                              │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Group: osiris:enrichers     → 3 consumers (geo, IOC, metadata)      │   │\n│  │  Group: osiris:scorers       → 2 consumers (risk, importance)        │   │\n│  │  Group: osiris:correlators   → 2 consumers (spatial, temporal)       │   │\n│  │  Group: osiris:graph-writers → 1 consumer (Neo4j batch writes)       │   │\n│  │  Group: osiris:ai-analysts   → 1 consumer (Gemini API calls)         │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  PUB/SUB CHANNELS (Notification temps réel)                          │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  channel: osiris:events     → Broadcast tous les événements          │   │\n│  │  channel: osiris:alerts     → Broadcast alertes critiques            │   │\n│  │  channel: osiris:heartbeat  → Health check des services              │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 1.2 Message Schema\n\n```typescript\n// ── Message brut (entrée seeders) ──\ninterface RawEvent {\n  id: string;                    // UUID v4\n  source: string;                // 'opensky' | 'usgs' | 'firms' | ...\n  source_type: string;           // 'adsb' | 'ais' | 'quake' | 'fire' | ...\n  timestamp: string;             // ISO 8601\n  data: Record<string, unknown>; // Payload spécifique à la source\n  received_at: string;           // Timestamp d'ingestion\n}\n\n// ── Message enrichi (après processing) ──\ninterface EnrichedEvent extends RawEvent {\n  geo?: {\n    lat: number;\n    lon: number;\n    accuracy: number;            // 0-100\n    place_name?: string;         // Reverse geocoded\n    country?: string;\n    region?: string;\n  };\n  ioc?: {\n    ips: string[];\n    domains: string[];\n    hashes: string[];\n    urls: string[];\n  };\n  metadata: {\n    confidence: number;          // 0-100\n    severity: 'LOW' | 'MEDIUM' | 'HIGH' | 'CRITICAL';\n    tags: string[];\n    enriched_at: string;\n  };\n}\n\n// ── Message intelligence (après scoring + corrélation) ──\ninterface IntelEvent extends EnrichedEvent {\n  score: {\n    importance: number;          // 0-100\n    risk: number;                // 0-100\n    confidence: number;          // 0-100\n    final: number;               // Score composite\n    severity: 'LOW' | 'ELEVATED' | 'HIGH' | 'CRITICAL';\n  };\n  correlations: Array<{\n    event_id: string;\n    type: string;                // 'spatial' | 'temporal' | 'causal'\n    strength: number;            // 0-100\n    description: string;\n  }>;\n  anomalies: Array<{\n    type: string;\n    severity: string;\n    description: string;\n  }>;\n  agent_insight?: {\n    type: 'insight' | 'alert' | 'anomaly';\n    summary: string;\n    reasoning: string;\n    recommended_actions: string[];\n    emitted_at: string;\n  };\n}\n```\n\n### 1.3 Consumer Group Implementation\n\n```typescript\n// ── Consumer générique Redis Stream ──\nclass StreamConsumer {\n  private redis: Redis;\n  private group: string;\n  private consumer: string;\n  private stream: string;\n  private batchSize: number = 100;\n  private pollIntervalMs: number = 100;\n\n  constructor(stream: string, group: string, consumer: string) {\n    this.redis = new Redis(process.env.REDIS_URL!);\n    this.stream = stream;\n    this.group = group;\n    this.consumer = consumer;\n  }\n\n  async start(handler: (events: RawEvent[]) => Promise<void>) {\n    // Créer le consumer group (idempotent)\n    try {\n      await this.redis.xgroup('CREATE', this.stream, this.group, '$', 'MKSTREAM');\n    } catch (e: any) {\n      if (!e.message.includes('BUSYGROUP')) throw e;\n    }\n\n    // Boucle de consommation\n    while (true) {\n      const results = await this.redis.xreadgroup(\n        'GROUP', this.group, this.consumer,\n        'COUNT', this.batchSize,\n        'BLOCK', this.pollIntervalMs,\n        'STREAMS', this.stream, '>'\n      );\n\n      if (!results || results.length === 0) continue;\n\n      const events: RawEvent[] = [];\n      const ids: string[] = [];\n\n      for (const [, messages] of results) {\n        for (const [id, fields] of messages) {\n          events.push(JSON.parse(fields[1])); // fields[0] = 'data', fields[1] = JSON\n          ids.push(id);\n        }\n      }\n\n      try {\n        await handler(events);\n        // Ack les messages traités\n        await this.redis.xack(this.stream, this.group, ...ids);\n      } catch (error) {\n        console.error(`[${this.consumer}] Processing failed:`, error);\n        // Les messages non-ack seront re-livrés\n      }\n    }\n  }\n}\n\n// ── Utilisation ──\nconst enricher = new StreamConsumer('osiris:raw:opensky', 'osiris:enrichers', 'geo-enricher-1');\nenricher.start(async (events) => {\n  for (const event of events) {\n    const enriched = await enrichWithGeo(event);\n    await redis.xadd('osiris:enriched', '*', 'data', JSON.stringify(enriched));\n  }\n});\n```\n\n### 1.4 Event Processing Pipeline\n\n```\n┌──────────┐   ┌──────────┐   ┌──────────┐   ┌──────────┐   ┌──────────┐\n│  STAGE 1  │   │  STAGE 2  │   │  STAGE 3  │   │  STAGE 4  │   │  STAGE 5  │\n│ VALIDATION│──▶│ENRICHMENT│──▶│ SCORING  │──▶│CORRELATION│──▶│ DELIVERY │\n│          │   │          │   │          │   │          │   │          │\n│ • Schema │   │ • Geo    │   │ • Risk   │   │ • Spatial │   │ • WS     │\n│ • Dedup  │   │ • IOC    │   │ • Import │   │ • Temporel│   │ • Graph  │\n│ • Rate   │   │ • Meta   │   │ • Conf   │   │ • Causal  │   │ • AI     │\n└──────────┘   └──────────┘   └──────────┘   └──────────┘   └──────────┘\n     │              │              │              │              │\n     ▼              ▼              ▼              ▼              ▼\n┌──────────┐   ┌──────────┐   ┌──────────┐   ┌──────────┐   ┌──────────┐\n│osiris:raw│   │osiris:en-│   │osiris:   │   │osiris:   │   │osiris:   │\n│:{source} │   │riched    │   │scored    │   │intel     │   │alerts    │\n└──────────┘   └──────────┘   └──────────┘   └──────────┘   └──────────┘\n```\n\n---\n\n## 2. Modular Backend Services\n\n### 2.1 Service Architecture\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                         SERVICE MESH ARCHITECTURE GATEWAY (Node.js/Go)                                            │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐  │   │\n│  │  │ Auth     │ │ Rate     │ │ Router   │ │ WebSocket│ │ Cache    │  │   │\n│  │  │ Middleware│ │ Limiter  │ │ /api/*   │ │ Upgrader │ │ (Redis)  │  │   │\n│  │  └──────────┘ └──────────┘ └──────────┘ └──────────┘ └──────────┘  │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                    │ │  ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌──▼───────┐ ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐  │   │\n│  │  │ AI       │ │ GEOINT   │ │ THREAT   │ │ GRAPH    │ │ SEARCH   │  │   │\n│  │  │ ENGINE   │ │ ENGINE   │ │ ENGINE   │ │ ENGINE   │ │ ENGINE   │  │   │\n│  │  │ :5001    │ │ :5002    │ │ :5003    │ │ :5004    │ │ :5005    │  │   │\n│  │  └────┬─────┘ └────┬─────┘ └────┬─────┘ └────┬─────┘ └────┬─────┘  │   │\n│  │       │            │            │            │            │         │   │\n│  │  ┌────▼────────────▼────────────▼────────────▼────────────▼─────┐   │   │\n│  │  │                    REDIS EVENT BUS                            │   │   │\n│  │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 2.2 Service Specifications\n\n#### AI Engine (:5001)\n\n```typescript\n// Interface du service AI\ninterface AIEngineService {\n  // Analyse d'intelligence\n  analyzeIntelligence(context: IntelligenceContext, query: string): Promise<AnalysisResult>;\n  \n  // Briefing quotidien\n  generateBriefing(context: IntelligenceContext): Promise<BriefingResult>;\n  \n  // Dossier régional\n  generateRegionDossier(lat: number, lng: number): Promise<RegionDossier>;\n  \n  // Agent insight streaming\n  streamAgentInsights(events: IntelEvent[]): AsyncGenerator<AgentInsight>;\n}\n\n// Configuration\nconst AI_CONFIG = {\n  model: 'gemini-2.0-flash',\n  maxTokens: 8192,\n  temperature: 0.2,           // Faible pour analyse précise\n  topP: 0.95,\n  apiKeyRotation: true,       // Round-robin sur 8 clés\n  rateLimit: 5,               // Requêtes/minute\n  contextWindow: 1_000_000,   // Tokens\n};\n```\n\n#### GEOINT Engine (:5002)\n\n```typescript\ninterface GEOINTEngineService {\n  // Reverse geocoding\n  reverseGeocode(lat: number, lng: number): Promise<GeocodeResult>;\n  \n  // Calcul de distance (Haversine)\n  distance(a: Coord, b: Coord): number;\n  \n  // Bounding box filtering\n  filterByBbox<T extends GeoEntity>(items: T[], bbox: BBox): T[];\n  \n  // Clustering spatial\n  spatialCluster<T extends GeoEntity>(items: T[], radiusKm: number): Cluster<T>[];\n  \n  // Satellite pass prediction\n  predictPasses(tle: string, observer: Coord, days: number): PassPrediction[];\n  \n  // Tuiles proxy\n  proxyTile(url: string, z: number, x: number, y: number): Promise<Buffer>;\n}\n\n// Types\ninterface Coord { lat: number; lng: number; }\ninterface BBox { west: number; south: number; east: number; north: number; }\ninterface GeoEntity { lat: number; lng: number; [key: string]: unknown; }\ninterface Cluster<T> { center: Coord; items: T[]; radius: number; }\n```\n\n#### Threat Engine (:5003)\n\n```typescript\ninterface ThreatEngineService {\n  // Scoring CVE\n  scoreCVE(cve: CVERecord): ThreatScore;\n  \n  // Malware correlation\n  correlateMalware(ioc: IOC): CorrelationResult[];\n  \n  // KEV prioritization\n  prioritizeKEV(kevs: KEVRecord[]): PrioritizedKEV[];\n  \n  // Country risk assessment\n  assessCountryRisk(country: string): CountryRisk;\n  \n  // Sanctions screening\n  screenSanctions(entity: string): SanctionMatch[];\n}\n\n// Scoring algorithm\nfunction calculateThreatScore(cve: CVERecord): number {\n  const baseScore = cve.cvss_score || 0;\n  const kevBonus = cve.in_kev ? 25 : 0;\n  const exploitBonus = cve.has_exploit ? 15 : 0;\n  const agePenalty = Math.max(0, 30 - (Date.now() - cve.published) / (1000 * 86400));\n  \n  return Math.min(100, baseScore * 10 + kevBonus + exploitBonus - agePenalty);\n}\n```\n\n#### Graph Engine (:5004)\n\n```typescript\ninterface GraphEngineService {\n  // Entity resolution\n  resolveEntity(entity: Entity): ResolvedEntity;\n  \n  // Relation extraction\n  extractRelations(events: IntelEvent[]): Relation[];\n  \n  // Graph query\n  queryGraph(params: GraphQuery): GraphResult;\n  \n  // Entity expansion (1-2 hops)\n  expandEntity(id: string, hops: number): GraphData;\n  \n  // Batch write\n  writeBatch(operations: GraphOperation[]): Promise<void>;\n}\n\n// Types de nœuds\ntype EntityType = \n  | 'IP' | 'DOMAIN' | 'EMAIL' | 'PERSON' | 'ORGANIZATION'\n  | 'VESSEL' | 'AIRCRAFT' | 'COUNTRY' | 'CVE' | 'EVENT'\n  | 'IOC' | 'SANCTION' | 'INFRASTRUCTURE';\n\n// Types de relations\ntype RelationType =\n  | 'RESOLVES_TO'      // Domain → IP\n  | 'BELONGS_TO'       // Email → Person\n  | 'LOCATED_IN'       // Entity → Country\n  | 'CORRELATED_WITH'  // Event ↔ Event\n  | 'TARGETS'          // CVE → Product\n  | 'OBSERVED_IN'      // IOC → Country\n  | 'ASSOCIATED_WITH'  // Person → Organization\n  | 'OPERATES'         // Organization → Vessel/Aircraft\n  | 'SANCTIONED_BY';   // Entity → Sanction\n```\n\n#### Search Engine (:5005)\n\n```typescript\ninterface SearchEngineService {\n  // Full-text search\n  search(query: string, options: SearchOptions): SearchResults;\n  \n  // Index document\n  indexDocument(doc: SearchDocument): Promise<void>;\n  \n  // Batch index\n  batchIndex(docs: SearchDocument[]): Promise<void>;\n  \n  // Faceted search\n  facetedSearch(query: string, facets: Facet[]): FacetedResults;\n}\n\ninterface SearchDocument {\n  id: string;\n  type: EntityType;\n  title: string;\n  description: string;\n  content: string;\n  tags: string[];\n  geo?: { lat: number; lng: number };\n  timestamp: string;\n  source: string;\n  score: number;\n}\n```\n\n### 2.3 Service Discovery & Health\n\n```typescript\n// ── Module Registry ──\ninterface ModuleRegistration {\n  name: string;\n  version: string;\n  host: string;\n  port: number;\n  healthEndpoint: string;\n  capabilities: string[];\n  dependencies: string[];\n  status: 'ACTIVE' | 'DEGRADED' | 'OFFLINE';\n  lastHeartbeat: string;\n  metrics: {\n    uptime: number;\n    requestsTotal: number;\n    errorsTotal: number;\n    avgLatencyMs: number;\n  };\n}\n\n// ── Health Check Response ──\ninterface HealthResponse {\n  status: 'ok' | 'degraded' | 'error';\n  version: string;\n  uptime: number;\n  dependencies: Record<string, {\n    status: 'ok' | 'error';\n    latencyMs: number;\n  }>;\n  metrics: {\n    memory: { used: number; total: number };\n    cpu: number;\n    connections: number;\n  };\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Real-Time Data Processing\n\n### 3.1 WebSocket Architecture\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                      WEBSOCKET ARCHITECTURE                                 │\n│                                                                              │\n│  Client (Browser)                    Server (Next.js)                       │\n│  ┌──────────────────┐               ┌──────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │ useCoreBrainStream│               │  /core-brain/stream              │   │\n│  │                  │               │                                  │   │\n│  │  ┌────────────┐  │   WebSocket   │  ┌────────────┐ ┌────────────┐  │   │\n│  │  │ Queue      │──┼───────────────┼─▶│ Auth       │ │ Redis      │  │   │\n│  │  │ (flush 250ms)│  │               │  │ Middleware │ │ Subscriber │  │   │\n│  │  └────────────┘  │               │  └────────────┘ └─────┬──────┘  │   │\n│  │  ┌────────────┐  │               │                        │         │   │\n│  │  │ Dedup Map  │  │               │  ┌─────────────────────▼──────┐  │   │\n│  │  │ (by ID)    │  │               │  │  Event Router              │  │   │\n│  │  └────────────┘  │               │  │                            │  │   │\n│  │  ┌────────────┐  │               │  │  ┌──────────┐ ┌──────────┐ │  │   │\n│  │  │ Max 2000   │  │               │  │  │ Snapshot │ │ Stream   │ │  │   │\n│  │  │ Events     │  │               │  │  │ (on conn)│ │ (live)   │ │  │   │\n│  │  └────────────┘  │               │  │  └──────────┘ └──────────┘ │  │   │\n│  └──────────────────┘               │  └────────────────────────────┘  │   │\n│                                      └──────────────────────────────────┘   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 3.2 WebSocket Protocol\n\n```typescript\n// ── Messages Serveur → Client ──\ntype ServerMessage =\n  | { type: 'hello'; service: string; version: string; time: string }\n  | { type: 'snapshot'; events: IntelEvent[]; total: number; time: string }\n  | { type: 'intelligence.update'; intelligence: IntelEvent; time: string }\n  | { type: 'alert'; alert: IntelEvent; time: string }\n  | { type: 'insight'; insight: AgentInsight; time: string }\n  | { type: 'status'; status: 'connected' | 'reconnecting' | 'error'; time: string }\n  | { type: 'pong'; time: string };\n\n// ── Messages Client → Serveur ──\ntype ClientMessage =\n  | { type: 'subscribe'; channels: string[] }\n  | { type: 'unsubscribe'; channels: string[] }\n  | { type: 'ping' }\n  | { type: 'ack'; event_id: string };\n\n// ── Client Hook (useCoreBrainStream) ──\ninterface CoreBrainStreamState {\n  events: IntelEvent[];\n  status: 'connecting' | 'connected' | 'reconnecting' | 'error';\n  lastMessageAt: string | null;\n  stats: {\n    received: number;\n    buffered: number;\n    dropped: number;\n    retained: number;\n  };\n}\n\n// Stratégie de reconnexion exponentielle\nconst RECONNECT_STRATEGY = {\n  initialDelay: 750,      // ms\n  maxDelay: 30_000,       // ms\n  multiplier: 2,\n  jitter: 0.1,            // 10% jitter\n};\n```\n\n### 3.3 Polling Strategy\n\n```typescript\n// ── Configuration des intervalles de polling ──\nconst POLLING_CONFIG = {\n  // Temps réel (layer actif)\n  maritime: { interval: 10_000, priority: 1 },     // 10s\n  flights:  { interval: 120_000, priority: 2 },     // 2 min (layer actif)\n  \n  // Haute fréquence\n  cctv:     { interval: 300_000, priority: 3 },     // 5 min\n  markets:  { interval: 300_000, priority: 3 },     // 5 min\n  \n  // Basse fréquence\n  earthquakes: { interval: 900_000, priority: 4 },  // 15 min\n  news:        { interval: 1_800_000, priority: 5 }, // 30 min\n  satellites:  { interval: 3_600_000, priority: 6 }, // 1 heure\n  cyber:       { interval: 3_600_000, priority: 6 }, // 1 heure\n  \n  // À la demande (layer inactif)\n  default: { interval: null, priority: 7 },\n};\n\n// ── Layer-aware fetching ──\nclass LayerAwareFetcher {\n  private activeLayers: Set<string> = new Set();\n  private intervals: Map<string, NodeJS.Timeout> = new Map();\n  private fetchedRef: Set<string> = new Set();\n\n  activateLayer(layer: string) {\n    this.activeLayers.add(layer);\n    const config = POLLING_CONFIG[layer as keyof typeof POLLING_CONFIG];\n    \n    if (config && config.interval) {\n      // Premier fetch immédiat\n      if (!this.fetchedRef.has(layer)) {\n        this.fetchLayer(layer);\n        this.fetchedRef.add(layer);\n      }\n      \n      // Polling interval\n      const interval = setInterval(() => this.fetchLayer(layer), config.interval);\n      this.intervals.set(layer, interval);\n    }\n  }\n\n  deactivateLayer(layer: string) {\n    this.activeLayers.delete(layer);\n    const interval = this.intervals.get(layer);\n    if (interval) {\n      clearInterval(interval);\n      this.intervals.delete(layer);\n    }\n  }\n\n  private async fetchLayer(layer: string) {\n    try {\n      const response = await fetch(`/api/${layer}`, { cache: 'no-store' });\n      if (response.ok) {\n        const data = await response.json();\n        this.onData(layer, data);\n      }\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.warn(`[Fetcher] ${layer} failed:`, error);\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 3.4 Data Deduplication\n\n```typescript\n// ── Déduplication par ID avec Map ──\nclass EventDeduplicator {\n  private events: Map<string, IntelEvent> = new Map();\n  private maxSize: number;\n\n  constructor(maxSize: number = 2000) {\n    this.maxSize = maxSize;\n  }\n\n  add(event: IntelEvent): void {\n    if (!event.id) return;\n    \n    // Éviction LRU si dépassement\n    if (this.events.size >= this.maxSize) {\n      const oldestKey = this.events.keys().next().value;\n      if (oldestKey) this.events.delete(oldestKey);\n    }\n    \n    this.events.set(event.id, event);\n  }\n\n  addBatch(events: IntelEvent[]): void {\n    for (const event of events) {\n      this.add(event);\n    }\n  }\n\n  getAll(): IntelEvent[] {\n    return Array.from(this.events.values())\n      .sort((a, b) => {\n        const at = Date.parse(a.timestamp || a.source_event?.timestamp || '') || 0;\n        const bt = Date.parse(b.timestamp || b.source_event?.timestamp || '') || 0;\n        return at - bt;\n      });\n  }\n\n  getStats() {\n    return {\n      total: this.events.size,\n      maxSize: this.maxSize,\n      utilization: (this.events.size / this.maxSize * 100).toFixed(1) + '%',\n    };\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Intelligence Graph Core\n\n### 4.1 Graph Schema (Neo4j)\n\n```cypher\n// ── Contraintes d'unicité ──\nCREATE CONSTRAINT unique_entity IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Entity) REQUIRE e.id IS UNIQUE;\nCREATE CONSTRAINT unique_ip IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:IP) REQUIRE e.value IS UNIQUE;\nCREATE CONSTRAINT unique_domain IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Domain) REQUIRE e.value IS UNIQUE;\n\n// ── Index ──\nCREATE INDEX entity_type_idx IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Entity) ON (e.type);\nCREATE INDEX entity_score_idx IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Entity) ON (e.score);\nCREATE INDEX event_timestamp_idx IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Event) ON (e.timestamp);\n\n// ── Types de nœuds ──\n// :Entity { id, type, label, score, first_seen, last_seen, properties }\n// :IP { value, asn, country, isp, threat_score, first_seen }\n// :Domain { value, registrar, created, expires, threat_score }\n// :Email { address, domain, first_seen, breach_count }\n// :Person { name, aliases, nationality, role, confidence }\n// :Organization { name, ticker, sector, country, risk_score }\n// :Vessel { mmsi, imo, name, flag, type, tonnage }\n// :Aircraft { icao24, callsign, model, registration, operator }\n// :Country { iso3, name, region, risk_score, population }\n// :CVE { id, cvss_score, severity, published, in_kev, has_exploit }\n// :Event { id, type, title, severity, timestamp, lat, lon }\n// :IOC { type, value, threat_type, first_seen, source }\n// :Sanction { entity, regime, type, date, authority }\n// :Infrastructure { type, name, lat, lon, country, status }\n\n// ── Types de relations ──\n// (d:Domain)-[:RESOLVES_TO {first_seen, last_seen}]->(ip:IP)\n// (e:Email)-[:BELONGS_TO {confidence}]->(p:Person)\n// (e:Entity)-[:LOCATED_IN {source}]->(c:Country)\n// (e1:Event)-[:CORRELATED_WITH {strength, type}]->(e2:Event)\n// (c:CVE)-[:TARGETS {vector}]->(p:Product)\n// (i:IOC)-[:OBSERVED_IN {count}]->(c:Country)\n// (p:Person)-[:ASSOCIATED_WITH {role}]->(o:Organization)\n// (o:Organization)-[:OPERATES {since}]->(v:Vessel)\n// (e:Entity)-[:SANCTIONED_BY {date}]->(s:Sanction)\n```\n\n### 4.2 Entity Resolution Pipeline\n\n```typescript\n// ── Pipeline de résolution d'entités ──\nclass EntityResolver {\n  private matchers: EntityMatcher[] = [];\n  private neo4j: Driver;\n\n  constructor(neo4jUri: string) {\n    this.neo4j = neo4j.driver(neo4jUri);\n    this.registerMatchers();\n  }\n\n  private registerMatchers() {\n    // Matchers spécialisés par type\n    this.matchers.push(new ExactMatchMatcher());     // ID exact\n    this.matchers.push(new FuzzyNameMatcher());       // Nom approximatif\n    this.matchers.push(new AliasMatcher());           // Alias\n    this.matchers.push(new PropertyMatcher());        // Propriétés similaires\n    this.matchers.push(new TemporalMatcher());        // Même période\n    this.matchers.push(new SpatialMatcher());         // Même localisation\n  }\n\n  async resolve(entity: RawEntity): Promise<ResolvedEntity> {\n    // 1. Vérifier si l'entité existe déjà\n    const existing = await this.findExisting(entity);\n    if (existing) {\n      return this.merge(existing, entity);\n    }\n\n    // 2. Créer nouvelle entité\n    return this.create(entity);\n  }\n\n  private async findExisting(entity: RawEntity): Promise<Entity | null> {\n    const session = this.neo4j.session();\n    \n    try {\n      // Requête de matching multi-critères\n      const result = await session.run(`\n        MATCH (e:Entity)\n        WHERE e.type = $type\n        AND (\n          e.id = $id\n          OR ($name IS NOT NULL AND e.name CONTAINS $name)\n          OR ($props IS NOT NULL AND e.properties CONTAINS $props)\n        )\n        RETURN e\n        ORDER BY \n          CASE WHEN e.id = $id THEN 3\n               WHEN e.name = $name THEN 2\n               ELSE 1\n          END DESC\n        LIMIT 1\n      `, {\n        type: entity.type,\n        id: entity.id,\n        name: entity.name,\n        props: JSON.stringify(entity.properties),\n      });\n\n      return result.records[0]?.get('e')?.properties || null;\n    } finally {\n      await session.close();\n    }\n  }\n\n  private async merge(existing: Entity, incoming: RawEntity): Promise<ResolvedEntity> {\n    const session = this.neo4j.session();\n    \n    try {\n      // Fusionner les propriétés (nouveau > ancien)\n      const merged = {\n        ...existing.properties,\n        ...incoming.properties,\n        last_seen: new Date().toISOString(),\n        score: Math.max(existing.score || 0, incoming.score || 0),\n      };\n\n      // Mettre à jour dans Neo4j\n      await session.run(`\n        MATCH (e:Entity {id: $id})\n        SET e.properties = $merged,\n            e.last_seen = $lastSeen,\n            e.score = $score\n        RETURN e\n      `, {\n        id: existing.id,\n        merged: JSON.stringify(merged),\n        lastSeen: merged.last_seen,\n        score: merged.score,\n      });\n\n      return { id: existing.id, ...merged };\n    } finally {\n      await session.close();\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 4.3 Correlation Engine\n\n```typescript\n// ── Moteur de corrélation multi-domaine ──\nclass CorrelationEngine {\n  private correlators: Correlator[] = [];\n\n  constructor() {\n    this.correlators = [\n      new SpatialCorrelator(250),     // 250 km radius\n      new TemporalCorrelator(3600),   // 1 hour window\n      new CausalCorrelator(),         // Cause-effect patterns\n      new AttributeCorrelator(),      // Shared attributes\n    ];\n  }\n\n  async correlate(event: IntelEvent, context: IntelEvent[]): Promise<Correlation[]> {\n    const correlations: Correlation[] = [];\n\n    for (const other of context) {\n      if (other.id === event.id) continue;\n\n      for (const correlator of this.correlators) {\n        const result = correlator.analyze(event, other);\n        if (result) {\n          correlations.push(result);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Trier par force de corrélation\n    return correlations.sort((a, b) => b.strength - a.strength).slice(0, 10);\n  }\n}\n\n// ── Corrélateur Spatial ──\nclass SpatialCorrelator implements Correlator {\n  constructor(private maxRadiusKm: number) {}\n\n  analyze(a: IntelEvent, b: IntelEvent): Correlation | null {\n    const aGeo = a.geo;\n    const bGeo = b.geo;\n    if (!aGeo || !bGeo) return null;\n\n    const distance = this.haversine(aGeo.lat, aGeo.lon, bGeo.lat, bGeo.lon);\n    if (distance > this.maxRadiusKm) return null;\n\n    const strength = Math.max(0, 100 - (distance / this.maxRadiusKm) * 100);\n    \n    return {\n      event_id: b.id,\n      type: 'spatial',\n      strength: Math.round(strength),\n      description: `${Math.round(distance)} km de distance`,\n    };\n  }\n\n  private haversine(lat1: number, lon1: number, lat2: number, lon2: number): number {\n    const R = 6371;\n    const dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180;\n    const dLon = (lon2 - lon1) * Math.PI / 180;\n    const a = Math.sin(dLat/2)**2 + Math.cos(lat1 * Math.PI/180) * Math.cos(lat2 * Math.PI/180) * Math.sin(dLon/2)**2;\n    return 2 * R * Math.asin(Math.sqrt(a));\n  }\n}\n\n// ── Corrélateur Temporel ──\nclass TemporalCorrelator implements Correlator {\n  constructor(private windowSeconds: number) {}\n\n  analyze(a: IntelEvent, b: IntelEvent): Correlation | null {\n    const aTime = Date.parse(a.timestamp || '');\n    const bTime = Date.parse(b.timestamp || '');\n    if (!aTime || !bTime) return null;\n\n    const diff = Math.abs(aTime - bTime) / 1000;\n    if (diff > this.windowSeconds) return null;\n\n    const strength = Math.max(0, 100 - (diff / this.windowSeconds) * 100);\n    \n    return {\n      event_id: b.id,\n      type: 'temporal',\n      strength: Math.round(strength),\n      description: `${Math.round(diff)}s d'écart temporel`,\n    };\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 4.4 Intelligence Scoring\n\n```typescript\n// ── Scoreur d'intelligence ──\nclass IntelligenceScorer {\n  score(event: EnrichedEvent): IntelEvent['score'] {\n    const importance = this.calculateImportance(event);\n    const risk = this.calculateRisk(event);\n    const confidence = this.calculateConfidence(event);\n    const final = Math.round(importance * 0.4 + risk * 0.35 + confidence * 0.25);\n    \n    return {\n      importance: Math.min(100, importance),\n      risk: Math.min(100, risk),\n      confidence: Math.min(100, confidence),\n      final: Math.min(100, final),\n      severity: this.determineSeverity(final),\n    };\n  }\n\n  private calculateImportance(event: EnrichedEvent): number {\n    let score = 0;\n    \n    // Magnitude pour les séismes\n    if (event.source_type === 'quake') {\n      const mag = event.data.magnitude as number || 0;\n      score += mag * 10;\n      if (event.data.tsunami) score += 25;\n      if ((event.data.depth as number || 0) < 35) score += 10;\n    }\n    \n    // Sévérité pour les alertes météo\n    if (event.source_type === 'weather') {\n      const severity = (event.data.severity as string || '').toLowerCase();\n      score += severity === 'extreme' ? 80 : severity === 'severe' ? 60 : 30;\n    }\n    \n    // Score de risque pour les news\n    if (event.source_type === 'news') {\n      score += (event.data.risk_score as number || 0) * 8;\n    }\n    \n    // Bonus pour les événements géolocalisés\n    if (event.geo) score += 10;\n    \n    // Bonus pour les IOC détectés\n    if (event.ioc && (event.ioc.ips.length > 0 || event.ioc.domains.length > 0)) {\n      score += 15;\n    }\n    \n    return score;\n  }\n\n  private calculateRisk(event: EnrichedEvent): number {\n    let score = 0;\n    \n    // Risque basé sur le type de source\n    const sourceRisk: Record<string, number> = {\n      cyber: 80, malware: 75, quake: 60, fire: 55,\n      weather: 50, news: 40, adsb: 30, ais: 25,\n    };\n    score += sourceRisk[event.source_type] || 20;\n    \n    // Ajustement par sévérité\n    const severityMultiplier: Record<string, number> = {\n      CRITICAL: 1.5, HIGH: 1.3, MEDIUM: 1.1, LOW: 0.8,\n    };\n    score *= severityMultiplier[event.metadata.severity] || 1;\n    \n    // Pénalité pour faible confiance\n    if (event.metadata.confidence < 50) score *= 0.7;\n    \n    return score;\n  }\n\n  private calculateConfidence(event: EnrichedEvent): number {\n    let score = 50; // Base\n    \n    // Source fiable\n    const trustedSources = ['usgs', 'opensky', 'cisa', 'nasa'];\n    if (trustedSources.includes(event.source)) score += 20;\n    \n    // Données géolocalisées\n    if (event.geo) score += 10;\n    \n    // Timestamp récent\n    const age = Date.now() - Date.parse(event.timestamp);\n    if (age < 300_000) score += 10;       // < 5 min\n    else if (age < 3_600_000) score += 5; // < 1 heure\n    \n    // IOC vérifiés\n    if (event.ioc && event.ioc.ips.length > 0) score += 10;\n    \n    return score;\n  }\n\n  private determineSeverity(finalScore: number): 'LOW' | 'ELEVATED' | 'HIGH' | 'CRITICAL' {\n    if (finalScore >= 85) return 'CRITICAL';\n    if (finalScore >= 70) return 'HIGH';\n    if (finalScore >= 50) return 'ELEVATED';\n    return 'LOW';\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Frontend Dashboard Structure\n\n### 5.1 Component Tree\n\n```\n<RootLayout>\n  <ErrorBoundary>\n    <Dashboard>\n      │\n      ├── <SplashScreen />                    # Animation démarrage (2.5s)\n     │   ├── <SystemStatus />                # Backend + Core Brain status\n      │   ├── <SolarWeather />                # Kp index\n      │   └── <UptimeClock />                 # Uptime session\n tracks\n      │   │   ├── <MaritimeLayer />           # AIS vessels\n      │   │   ├── <SatelliteLayer />          # TLE positions\n      │   │   ├── <CCTVLayer />               # Camera markers\n <MapControls>                   # Zoom, projection, style\n      │       ├── <ProjectionToggle />        # Globe / Mercator\n      │       └── <StyleToggle />             # Dark / Satellite\n      │\n <LayerToggle name=\"earthquakes\" />\n      │   │   ├── <LayerToggle name=\"fires\" />\n      │   │   ├── <LayerToggle name=\"weather\" />\n      │   │   └── <LayerToggle name=\"global_incidents\" />\n      │   ├── <LayerGroup label=\"Infrastructure\">\n      │   │   ├── <LayerToggle name=\"cctv\" />\n      │   │   ├── <LayerToggle name=\"infrastructure\" />\n      │   │   └── <LayerToggle <ToolStrip>                         # Barre d'outils droite\n      │   ├── <ToolButton icon={Radar} panel=\"intel\" />\n      │   ├── <ToolButton icon={BarChart3} panel=\"markets\" />\n      │   ├── <ToolButton icon={AlertTriangle} panel=\"alerts\" />\n      │   ├── <ToolButton icon={SlidersHorizontal} panel=\"filters\" />\n      │   └── <ToolButton icon={Network} panel=\"graph\" />\n      │\n # CCTV stream (modal)\n      ├── <LiveFeedViewer />                  # YouTube/news stream (modal)\n      ├── <RegionDossier />                   # AI region brief (modal)\n      ├── <SharePanel /> dataVersion: number;\n  \n  // Carte\n  mapView: { zoom: number; latitude: number; longitude: number };\n  mapProjection: 'globe' | 'mercator';\n  mapStyle: 'dark' | 'satellite';\n  flyToLocation: { lat: number; lng: number; ts: number } | null;\n  \n  // Calques\n  activeLayers: Record<LayerName, boolean>;\n  \n  // Panneaux\n  showLayers: boolean;\n  showMarkets: boolean;\n  showAlerts: boolean;\n  showIntel: boolean;\n  showEntityGraph: boolean;\n  showModules: boolean;\n  showScmPanel: boolean;\n  \n  // Entité sélectionnée\n  entityGraphTarget: EntityTarget | null;\n  activeCamera: any | null;\n  liveFeedUrl: string | null;\n  \n  // Intelligence\n  eventFilters: EventFilterState;\n  filteredCoreEvents: IntelEvent[];\n  regionDossier: any | null;\n  \n  // Système\n  backendStatus: 'connecting' | 'connected' | 'error';\n  coreBrainStatus: CoreBrainStreamStatus;\n  spaceWeather: any | null;\n  globalStats: any | null;\n  \n  // UI\n  isMobile: boolean;\n  isFullscreen: boolean;\n  showSplash: boolean;\n  mobilePanel: string | null;\n  demoMode: boolean;\n}\n\n// ── Hooks personnalisés ──\nfunction useDashboardState(): DashboardState {\n  // useState pour chaque groupe\n  const [data, setData] = useState<Record<string, unknown[]>>({});\n  const [mapView, setMapView] = useState({ zoom: 6.5, latitude: 42.70, longitude: 25.48 });\n  const [activeLayers, setActiveLayers] = useState(DEFAULT_LAYERS);\n  const [eventFilters, setEventFilters] = useState(DEFAULT_EVENT_FILTERS);\n  \n  // WebSocket stream\n  const coreBrainStream = useCoreBrainStream({ maxEvents: 2000, flushMs: 250 });\n  \n  // Événements filtrés\n  const filteredCoreEvents = useMemo(\n    () => filterIntelligenceEvents(coreBrainStream.events, eventFilters),\n    [coreBrainStream.events, eventFilters]\n  );\n  \n  // URL state persistence\n  useEffect(() => {\n    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);\n    // Restaurer lat/lon/zoom/layers depuis l'URL\n  }, []);\n  \n  // Mise à jour URL\n  useEffect(() => {\n    // Debounced update de l'URL avec les paramètres actuels\n  }, [mapView, activeLayers]);\n  \n  return {\n    data, mapView, activeLayers, eventFilters,\n    filteredCoreEvents, coreBrainStream,\n    // ... autres états\n  };\n}\n```\n\n### 5.3 Performance Optimizations\n\n```typescript\n// ── Virtualisation du rendu carte ──\nconst MAP_PERFORMANCE_CONFIG = {\n  maxFeatures: 2000,           // Nombre max de features sur la carte\n  fps: 18,                     // FPS cible (limit pour économie batterie)\n  clusterRadius: 50,           // Rayon de clustering (pixels)\n  clusterMaxZoom: 12,          // Zoom max pour clustering\n  tileCacheSize: 64,           // Taille du cache tuiles (MB)\n  animationFramerate: 30,      // Framerate animations\n  throttleInterval: 100,       // Throttle events (ms)\n};\n\n// ── Chargement progressif ──\nconst PROGRESSIVE_LOADING = {\n  priority1: ['earthquakes', 'news', 'markets'],     // Chargé immédiatement\n  priority2: ['space-weather'],                       // Chargé après 5s\n  priority3: ['flights', 'satellites', 'maritime'],   // Chargé à l'activation du layer\n  priority4: ['cctv', 'fires', 'weather'],            // Chargé à l'activation\n};\n\n// ── Cache géocode LRU ──\nclass GeocodeCache {\n  private cache: Map<string, string> = new Map();\n  private maxSize: number = 500;\n  \n  get(key: string): string | undefined {\n    return this.cache.get(key);\n  }\n  \n  set(key: string, value: string): void {\n    if (this.cache.size >= this.maxSize) {\n      const oldest = this.cache.keys().next().value;\n      if (oldest) this.cache.delete(oldest);\n    }\n    this.cache.set(key, value);\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Inter-Module Communication\n\n### 6.1 Communication Patterns\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                      COMMUNICATION PATTERNS                                  │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  1. SYNCHRONE (REST)                                                │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Client ──GET/POST──▶ Gateway ──▶ Service ──▶ Response              │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Utilisation: Queries utilisateur, recherche, CRUD                   │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  2. ASYNCHRONE (Redis Streams)                                      │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Seeder ──xadd──▶ Stream ──xreadgroup──▶ Consumer ──xadd──▶ Next    │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Utilisation: Pipeline de traitement, ingestion, enrichment          │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  3. TEMPS RÉEL (WebSocket)                                           │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Client ◀──ws.send──▶ Server ◀──subscribe──▶ Redis Pub/Sub          │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Utilisation: Dashboard temps réel, alertes, notifications           │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  4. ÉVÉNEMENTIEL (Pub/Sub)                                           │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Publisher ──publish──▶ Channel ──subscribe──▶ Subscriber            │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Utilisation: Broadcast, heartbeat, notifications système            │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 6.2 Module API Contracts\n\n```typescript\n// ── Contrat d'interface entre modules ──\ninterface ModuleContract {\n  // Métadonnées\n  name: string;\n  version: string;\n  \n  // Endpoints REST\n  endpoints: {\n    path: string;\n    method: 'GET' | 'POST' | 'PUT' | 'DELETE';\n    request: unknown;\n    response: unknown;\n    rateLimit: number;\n  }[];\n  \n  // Streams Redis\n  streams: {\n    input: string[];    // Streams consommés\n    output: string[];   // Streams produits\n  };\n  \n  // Événements Pub/Sub\n  events: {\n    publishes: string[];\n    subscribes: string[];\n  };\n  \n  // Dépendances\n  dependencies: {\n    module: string;\n    version: string;\n    required: boolean;\n  }[];\n}\n\n// ── Exemple: Contrat du module AI ──\nconst AI_MODULE_CONTRACT: ModuleContract = {\n  name: 'ai-engine',\n  version: '1.0.0',\n  endpoints: [\n    {\n      path: '/api/ai/analyze',\n      method: 'POST',\n      request: { context: IntelligenceContext, query: string },\n      response: { analysis: string, confidence: number },\n      rateLimit: 5,\n    },\n    {\n      path: '/api/ai/briefing',\n      method: 'POST',\n      request: { context: IntelligenceContext },\n      response: { briefing: string, generated_at: string },\n      rateLimit: 2,\n    },\n  ],\n  streams: {\n    input: ['osiris:intel'],\n    output: ['osiris:insights'],\n  },\n  events: {\n    publishes: ['osiris:insights'],\n    subscribes: ['osiris:alerts'],\n  },\n  dependencies: [\n    { module: 'redis', version: '7.x', required: true },\n  ],\n};\n```\n\n### 6.3 Service Mesh (Internal)\n\n```typescript\n// ── Service Mesh interne (Docker network) ──\n// Tous les services communiquent via le réseau Docker osiris-net\n// Les appels inter-services utilisent les noms de conteneur comme hostname\n\n// Exemple: AI Engine appelle Graph Engine\nconst GRAPH_ENGINE_URL = 'http://graph:5004';\n\nasync function expandEntity(type: string, id: string): Promise<GraphData> {\n  const response = await fetch(`${GRAPH_ENGINE_URL}/api/entity/expand`, {\n    method: 'POST',\n    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n    body: JSON.stringify({ type, id, hops: 2 }),\n  });\n  \n  if (!response.ok) {\n    throw new Error(`Graph Engine error: ${response.status}`);\n  }\n  \n  return response.json();\n}\n\n// ── Circuit Breaker Pattern ──\nclass CircuitBreaker {\n  private failures: number = 0;\n  private threshold: number = 5;\n  private timeout: number = 30_000;\n  private lastFailure: number = 0;\n  private state: 'CLOSED' | 'OPEN' | 'HALF_OPEN' = 'CLOSED';\n\n  async call<T>(fn: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {\n    if (this.state === 'OPEN') {\n      if (Date.now() - this.lastFailure > this.timeout) {\n        this.state = 'HALF_OPEN';\n      } else {\n        throw new Error('Circuit breaker is OPEN');\n      }\n    }\n\n    try {\n      const result = await fn();\n      this.failures = 0;\n      this.state = 'CLOSED';\n      return result;\n    } catch (error) {\n      this.failures++;\n      this.lastFailure = Date.now();\n      \n      if (this.failures >= this.threshold) {\n        this.state = 'OPEN';\n      }\n      \n      throw error;\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7.  │  │osiris:raw│   │osiris:en-│   │osiris:   │   │osiris:   │          │  │\n│  │  │:{source} │   │riched    │   │scored    │   │intel     │          │  │\n│  │  └──────────┘   └──────────┘
