│\n│                    ┌───────────────┼───────────────┐                        │\n│                    ▼               ▼               ▼                        │\n│  ┌──────────────────┐  ┌──────────────────┐  ┌──────────────────┐         │\n│  │   GRAPH ENGINE   │  │   THREAT ENGINE  │  │    AI ENGINE     │         │\n│  │                  │  │                  │  │                  │         │\n│  │  Neo4j write     │  │  IOC enrichment  │  │  Gemini analysis │         │\n│  │  Entity resolve  │  │  CVE scoring     │  │  Briefing gen    │         │\n│  │  Relation link   │  │  Alert trigger   │  │  Agent insight   │         │\n│  └────────┬─────────┘  └────────┬─────────┘  └────────┬─────────┘         │\n│           │                     │                     │                    │\n│           ▼                     ▼                     ▼                    │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐  │\n│  │                    DELIVERY LAYER                                     │  │\n│  │                                                                       │  │\n│  │  ┌──────────┐   ┌──────────┐   ┌──────────┐   ┌──────────┐          │  │\n│  │  │WebSocket │   │  REST    │   │  GraphQL │   │  Webhook │          │  │\n│  │  │ (Stream) │   │  (Query) │   │  (Alt)   │   │  (Ext)   │          │  │\n│  │  └────┬─────┘   └──────────┘   └──────────┘   └──────────┘          │  │\n│  │       │                                                               │  │\n│  │       ▼                                                               │  │\n│  │  ┌──────────┐                                                        │  │\n│  │  │ FRONTEND │                                                        │  │\n│  │  │Dashboard │                                                        │  │\n│  │  └──────────┘                                                        │  │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘  │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 7.2 Data Flow by Event Type\n\n```typescript\n// ── ADS-B Flight Data Flow ──\nconst FLIGHT_DATA_FLOW = {\n  source: 'OpenSky Network API',\n  seeder: 'seeds/aviation.ts',\n  polling: '5 min (2 min si layer actif)',\n  format: {\n    icao24: string,      // Identifiant unique aéronef\n    callsign: string,    // Indicatif vol\n    lat: number,         // Latitude\n    lng: number,         // Longitude\n    altitude: number,    // Altitude (m)\n    speed: number,       // Vitesse (m/s)\n    heading: number,     // Cap (degrés)\n    on_ground: boolean,  // Au sol ?\n    category: string,    // Catégorie (military, private, etc.)\n  },\n  processing: [\n    'Validation (ICAO24 format, coordonnées valides)',\n    'Enrichissement (catégorie, opérateur, modèle)',\n    'Détection anomalies (GPS jamming, cluster militaire)',\n    'Corrélation spatiale (proximité conflits, météo)',\n  ],\n  storage: [\n    'Redis cache (5 min TTL)',\n    'Neo4j (nœud Aircraft + relations)',\n  ],\n  delivery: [\n    'WebSocket → Dashboard (points animés)',\n    'REST API → /api/flights',\n  ],\n};\n\n// ── Earthquake Data Flow ──\nconst EARTHQUAKE_DATA_FLOW = {\n  source: 'USGS Earthquake API',\n  seeder: 'seeds/earthquakes.ts',\n  polling: '15 min',\n  format: {\n    id: string,\n    magnitude: number,\n    place: string,\n    lat: number,\n    lng: number,\n    depth: number,\n    time: string,\n    tsunami: boolean,\n    alert: string | null,\n    felt: number | null,\n  },\n  processing: [\n    'Scoring magnitude (M6+ = CRITICAL)',\n    'Détection tsunami',\n    'Analyse clustering spatial',\n    'Corrélation infrastructure (ports <250km)',\n    'Corrélation news/météo (<300km)',\n  ],\n  storage: [\n    'Redis cache (15 min TTL)',\n    'Neo4j (nœud Event + relations)',\n  ],\n  delivery: [\n    'WebSocket → Dashboard (cercles proportionnels)',\n    'REST API → /api/earthquakes',\n    'Alerte si M6+ ou tsunami',\n  ],\n};\n```\n\n### 7.3 Data Flow Diagram (Mermaid)\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n    %% Sources\n    OpenSky[OpenSky ADS-B] -->|HTTP Poll| SeederAv[Seeder Aviation]\n    USGS[USGS Seismic] -->|HTTP Poll| SeederEq[Seeder Earthquakes]\n    NASA[NASA FIRMS] -->|HTTP Poll| SeederFire[Seeder Fires]\n    CISA[CISA KEV] -->|HTTP Poll| SeederCyber[Seeder Cyber]\n    RSS[RSS Feeds] -->|HTTP Poll| SeederNews[Seeder News]\n    \n    %% Ingestion\n    SeederAv -->|xadd| RawAv[osiris:raw:aviation]\n    SeederEq -->|xadd| RawEq[osiris:raw:earthquakes]\n    SeederFire -->|xadd| RawFire[osiris:raw:fires]\n    SeederCyber -->|xadd| RawCyber[osiris:raw:cyber]\n    SeederNews -->|xadd| RawNews[osiris:raw:news]\n    \n    %% Processing Pipeline\n    RawAv -->|xreadgroup| Validator[Validation & Dedup]\n    RawEq -->|xreadgroup| Validator\n    RawFire -->|xreadgroup| Validator\n    RawCyber -->|xreadgroup| Validator\n    RawNews -->|xreadgroup| Validator\n    \n    Validator -->|xadd| Enriched[osiris:enriched]\n    Enriched -->|xreadgroup| Enricher[Geo & IOC Enrichment]\n    Enricher -->|xadd| Scored[osiris:scored]\n    Scored -->|xreadgroup| Scorer[Risk & Importance Scoring]\n    Scorer -->|xadd| Intel[osiris:intel]\n    \n    %% Parallel Processing\n    Intel -->|xreadgroup| Correlator[Correlation Engine]\n    Intel -->|xreadgroup| GraphWriter[Graph Engine Writer]\n    Intel -->|xreadgroup| AIAnalyzer[AI Analysis]\n    \n    %% Outputs\n    Correlator -->|xadd| Alerts[osiris:alerts]\n    GraphWriter -->|batch write| Neo4j[(Neo4j Graph DB)]\n    AIAnalyzer -->|xadd| Insights[osiris:insights]\n    \n    %% Delivery\n    Alerts -->|subscribe| WSServer[WebSocket Server]\n    Intel -->|subscribe| WSServer\n    Insights -->|subscribe| WSServer\n    \n    WSServer -->|ws.send| Dashboard[Frontend Dashboard]\n    Dashboard -->|REST| APIGateway[API Gateway]\n    APIGateway -->|query| Neo4j\n    APIGateway -->|query| Meili[(Meilisearch)]\n    APIGateway -->|query| Redis[(Redis Cache)]\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Storage Architecture\n\n### 8.1 Storage Layer Overview\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                         STORAGE ARCHITECTURE                                │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  REDIS (Cache + Event Bus + Session)                                 │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐               │   │\n│  │  │ Streams      │  │ Cache        │  │ Session      │               │   │\n│  │  │ (Event Bus)  │  │ (TTL 5-60min)│  │ (JWT + State)│               │   │\n│  │  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘               │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  NEO4J (Graph Database)                                              │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐               │   │\n│  │  │ Entities     │  │ Relations    │  │ Indexes      │               │   │\n│  │  │ (10 types)   │  │ (8 types)    │  │ (type, score)│               │   │\n│  │  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘               │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  MEILISEARCH (Full-Text Search)                                      │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐               │   │\n│  │  │ Documents    │  │ Indexes      │  │ Facets       │               │   │\n│  │  │ (Entities)   │  │ (type, geo)  │  │ (filters)    │               │   │\n│  │  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘               │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  MINIO (Object Storage - S3 Compatible)                              │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐               │   │\n│  │  │ Images       │  │ Reports      │  │ Exports      │               │   │\n│  │  │ (CCTV, Maps) │  │ (PDF, CSV)   │  │ (STIX, JSON) │               │   │\n│  │  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘               │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 8.2 Redis Schema\n\n```typescript\n// ── Redis Key Naming Convention ──\n// {namespace}:{type}:{id}\n\nconst REDIS_KEYS = {\n  // Streams (Event Bus)\n  streams: {\n    raw: (source: string) => `osiris:raw:${source}`,\n    enriched: 'osiris:enriched',\n    scored: 'osiris:scored',\n    intel: 'osiris:intel',\n    alerts: 'osiris:alerts',\n    insights: 'osiris:insights',\n    graph: 'osiris:graph',\n  },\n  \n  // Cache\n  cache: {\n    flights: 'cache:flights',\n    satellites: 'cache:satellites',\n    maritime: 'cache:maritime',\n    earthquakes: 'cache:earthquakes',\n    news: 'cache:news',\n    markets: 'cache:markets',\n    geocode: (lat: number, lng: number) => `cache:geo:${lat.toFixed(1)}:${lng.toFixed(1)}`,\n  },\n  \n  // Session\n  session: {\n    user: (id: string) => `session:user:${id}`,\n    jwt: (jti: string) => `session:jwt:${jti}`,\n    rateLimit: (ip: string, endpoint: string) => `ratelimit:${ip}:${endpoint}`,\n  },\n  \n  // Pub/Sub\n  pubsub: {\n    events: 'osiris:events',\n    alerts: 'osiris:alerts',\n    heartbeat: 'osiris:heartbeat',\n  },\n};\n\n// ── Cache TTL Configuration ──\nconst CACHE_TTL = {\n  flights: 300,           // 5 min\n  satellites: 3600,       // 1 heure\n  maritime: 60,           // 1 min\n  earthquakes: 900,       // 15 min\n  news: 1800,             // 30 min\n  markets: 300,           // 5 min\n  geocode: 86400,         // 24 heures\n  session: 3600,          // 1 heure\n  rateLimit: 60,          // 1 min\n};\n```\n\n### 8.3 Neo4j Schema\n\n```cypher\n// ── Création des contraintes ──\nCREATE CONSTRAINT entity_id IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Entity) REQUIRE e.id IS UNIQUE;\nCREATE CONSTRAINT ip_value IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:IP) REQUIRE e.value IS UNIQUE;\nCREATE CONSTRAINT domain_value IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Domain) REQUIRE e.value IS UNIQUE;\nCREATE CONSTRAINT email_value IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Email) REQUIRE e.value IS UNIQUE;\nCREATE CONSTRAINT cve_id IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:CVE) REQUIRE e.id IS UNIQUE;\n\n// ── Création des index ──\nCREATE INDEX entity_type IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Entity) ON (e.type);\nCREATE INDEX entity_score IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Entity) ON (e.score);\nCREATE INDEX entity_last_seen IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Entity) ON (e.last_seen);\nCREATE INDEX event_timestamp IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Event) ON (e.timestamp);\nCREATE INDEX event_severity IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Event) ON (e.severity);\nCREATE INDEX event_location IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:Event) ON (e.lat, e.lon);\n\n// ── Requêtes typiques ──\n\n// Expansion d'entité (1-2 hops)\nMATCH (e:Entity {id: $id})-[r*1..2]-(connected)\nRETURN e, r, connected\nLIMIT 100\n\n// Événements corrélés spatialement\nMATCH (e:Event {type: 'earthquake'})\nWHERE e.lat BETWEEN $south AND $north\n  AND e.lon BETWEEN $west AND $east\n  AND e.timestamp > $since\nRETURN e\nORDER BY e.score DESC\nLIMIT 50\n\n// Chemins entre deux entités\nMATCH path = shortestPath(\n  (a:Entity {id: $source})-[*..6]-(b:Entity {id: $target})\n)\nRETURN path\n\n// Entités les plus connectées\nMATCH (e:Entity)\nRETURN e.id, e.type, e.label, size((e)--()) AS connections\nORDER BY connections DESC\nLIMIT 20\n\n// Cluster d'IOC par pays\nMATCH (i:IOC)-[:OBSERVED_IN]->(c:Country)\nRETURN c.name, count(i) AS ioc_count, collect(i.value)[..5] AS samples\nORDER BY ioc_count DESC\n```\n\n### 8.4 Meilisearch Configuration\n\n```typescript\n// ── Configuration Meilisearch ──\nconst MEILISEARCH_CONFIG = {\n  host: process.env.MEILISEARCH_URL || 'http://search:7700',\n  apiKey: process.env.MEILISEARCH_API_KEY,\n  \n  indexes: {\n    entities: {\n      primaryKey: 'id',\n      searchableAttributes: ['title', 'description', 'content', 'tags'],\n      filterableAttributes: ['type', 'source', 'severity', 'country'],\n      sortableAttributes: ['score', 'timestamp'],\n      rankingRules: ['words', 'typo', 'proximity', 'attribute', 'sort', 'exactness'],\n    },\n  },\n};\n\n// ── Document type pour l'index ──\ninterface SearchDocument {\n  id: string;\n  type: string;           // EntityType\n  title: string;\n  description: string;\n  content: string;\n  tags: string[];\n  source: string;\n  severity: string;\n  country?: string;\n  score: number;\n  timestamp: string;\n  _geo?: {\n    lat: number;\n    lng: number;\n  };\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 9. Ingestion Pipeline\n\n### 9.1 Seeder Architecture\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                         SEEDER ARCHITECTURE                                 │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  SEEDER BASE CLASS                                                    │   │\n│  │                                                                       │   │\n│  │  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐             │   │\n│  │  │ Fetch    │  │ Parse    │  │ Validate │  │ Publish  │             │   │\n│  │  │ (HTTP)   │──▶│ (JSON/   │──▶│ (Schema) │──▶│ (Redis)  │             │   │\n│  │  │          │   │  XML)    │   │          │   │          │             │   │\n│  │  └──────────┘  └──────────┘  └──────────┘  └──────────┘             │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  SEEDERS IMPLÉMENTÉS (30+)                                           │   │\n│  │                                                                       │   │\n│  │  ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐│   │\n│  │  │ aviation.ts  │ │ maritime.ts  │ │ satellites.ts│ │ earthquakes  ││   │\n│  │  │ (OpenSky)    │ │ (AIS)        │ │ (Celestrak)  │ │ .ts (USGS)   ││   │\n│  │  └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘│   │\n│  │  ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐│   │\n│  │  │ fires.ts     │ │ weather.ts   │ │ news.ts      │ │ gdelt.ts     ││   │\n│  │  │ (FIRMS)      │ │ (NOAA)       │ │ (RSS)        │ │ (GDELT)      ││   │\n│  │  └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘│   │\n│  │  ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐│   │\n│  │  │ cyber.ts     │ │ malware.ts   │ │ cctv.ts      │ │ markets.ts   ││   │\n│  │  │ (CISA KEV)   │ │ (abuse.ch)   │ │ (18 pays)    │ │ (Finance)    ││   │\n│  │  └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘│   │\n│  │  ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐│   │\n│  │  │ space.ts     │ │ sentinel.ts  │ │ radar.ts     │ │ sanctions.ts ││   │\n│  │  │ (SpaceWx)    │ │ (ESA)        │ │ (IODA)       │ │ (OFAC/EU)    ││   │\n│  │  └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘│   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 9.2 Seeder Base Class\n\n```typescript\n// ── Seeder Base Class ──\nabstract class Seeder {\n  protected name: string;\n  protected redis: Redis;\n  protected stream: string;\n  protected pollInterval: number;\n  protected timer: NodeJS.Timeout | null = null;\n  protected isRunning: boolean = false;\n  protected stats: SeederStats = {\n    totalFetched: 0,\n    totalPublished: 0,\n    totalErrors: 0,\n    lastFetchAt: null,\n    lastErrorAt: null,\n    status: 'INITIALIZING',\n  };\n\n  constructor(config: SeederConfig) {\n    this.name = config.name;\n    this.redis = new Redis(process.env.REDIS_URL!);\n    this.stream = `osiris:raw:${config.name}`;\n    this.pollInterval = config.pollInterval;\n  }\n\n  // ── Template Method Pattern ──\n  async start(): Promise<void> {\n    this.isRunning = true;\n    this.stats.status = 'RUNNING';\n    \n    // Premier fetch immédiat\n    await this.fetchAndPublish();\n    \n    // Polling interval\n    this.timer = setInterval(() => {\n      if (this.isRunning) {\n        this.fetchAndPublish();\n      }\n    }, this.pollInterval);\n  }\n\n  stop(): void {\n    this.isRunning = false;\n    if (this.timer) {\n      clearInterval(this.timer);\n      this.timer = null;\n    }\n    this.stats.status = 'STOPPED';\n  }\n\n  private async fetchAndPublish(): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      this.stats.lastFetchAt = new Date().toISOString();\n      \n      // 1. Fetch data from source\n      const rawData = await this.fetch();\n      this.stats.totalFetched += Array.isArray(rawData) ? rawData.length : 1;\n      \n      // 2. Parse into normalized format\n      const events = await this.parse(rawData);\n      \n      // 3. Validate each event\n      const validEvents = events.filter(event => this.validate(event));\n      \n      // 4. Publish to Redis stream\n      for (const event of validEvents) {\n        await this.redis.xadd(\n          this.stream,\n          '*',\n          'data',\n          JSON.stringify({\n            ...event,\n            id: event.id || crypto.randomUUID(),\n            source: this.name,\n            received_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n          })\n        );\n      }\n      \n      this.stats.totalPublished += validEvents.length;\n      this.stats.status = 'RUNNING';\n      \n    } catch (error) {\n      this.stats.totalErrors++;\n      this.stats.lastErrorAt = new Date().toISOString();\n      this.stats.status = 'ERROR';\n      console.error(`[${this.name}] Fetch error:`, error);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // ── Méthodes à implémenter par chaque seeder ──\n  protected abstract fetch(): Promise<unknown>;\n  protected abstract parse(data: unknown): Promise<RawEvent[]>;\n  protected abstract validate(event: RawEvent): boolean;\n\n  getStats(): SeederStats {\n    return { ...this.stats };\n  }\n}\n\n// ── Exemple: Seeder Earthquakes ──\nclass EarthquakeSeeder extends Seeder {\n  constructor() {\n    super({\n      name: 'earthquakes',\n      pollInterval: 900_000, // 15 min\n    });\n  }\n\n  protected async fetch(): Promise<unknown> {\n    const response = await fetch(\n      'https://earthquake.usgs.gov/earthquakes/feed/v1.0/summary/2.5_day.geojson'\n    );\n    return response.json();\n  }\n\n  protected async parse(data: any): Promise<RawEvent[]> {\n    return data.features.map((feature: any) => ({\n      id: feature.id,\n      source_type: 'quake',\n      timestamp: new Date(feature.properties.time).toISOString(),\n      data: {\n        magnitude: feature.properties.mag,\n        place: feature.properties.place,\n        depth: feature.geometry.coordinates[2],\n        lat: feature.geometry.coordinates[1],\n        lng: feature.geometry.coordinates[0],\n        tsunami: feature.properties.tsunami === 1,\n        alert: feature.properties.alert,\n        felt: feature.properties.felt,\n        status: feature.properties.status,\n      },\n    }));\n  }\n\n  protected validate(event: RawEvent): boolean {\n    return (\n      typeof event.data.magnitude === 'number' &&\n      event.data.magnitude > 0 &&\n      typeof event.data.lat === 'number' &&\n      typeof event.data.lng === 'number' &&\n      Math.abs(event.data.lat) <= 90 &&\n      Math.abs(event.data.lng) <= 180\n    );\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 9.3 Pipeline Processing Stages\n\n```typescript\n// ── Stage 1: Validation & Deduplication ──\nclass ValidationStage {\n  private seenIds: Set<string> = new Set();\n  private maxIds: number = 100_000;\n\n  async process(events: RawEvent[]): Promise<RawEvent[]> {\n    const valid: RawEvent[] = [];\n\n    for (const event of events) {\n      // Validation basique\n      if (!event.id || !event.source || !event.timestamp) continue;\n      \n      // Déduplication\n      if (this.seenIds.has(event.id)) continue;\n      this.seenIds.add(event.id);\n      \n      // Éviction LRU\n      if (this.seenIds.size > this.maxIds) {\n        const first = this.seenIds.values().next().value;\n        if (first) this.seenIds.delete(first);\n      }\n      \n      valid.push(event);\n    }\n\n    return valid;\n  }\n}\n\n// ── Stage 2: Geo & IOC Enrichment ──\nclass EnrichmentStage {\n  private geocoder: Geocoder;\n  private iocExtractor: IOCExtractor;\n\n  constructor() {\n    this.geocoder = new NominatimGeocoder();\n    this.iocExtractor = new IOCExtractor();\n  }\n\n  async process(events: RawEvent[]): Promise<EnrichedEvent[]> {\n    const enriched: EnrichedEvent[] = [];\n\n    for (const event of events) {\n      const enrichedEvent: EnrichedEvent = {\n        ...event,\n        geo: await this.extractGeo(event),\n        ioc: this.iocExtractor.extract(event),\n        metadata: {\n          confidence: this.calculateConfidence(event),\n          severity: this.determineSeverity(event),\n          tags: this.extractTags(event),\n          enriched_at: new Date().toISOString(),\n        },\n      };\n\n      enriched.push(enrichedEvent);\n    }\n\n    return enriched;\n  }\n\n  private async extractGeo(event: RawEvent): Promise<EnrichedEvent['geo'] | undefined> {\n    // Vérifier si l'événement a déjà des coordonnées\n    const lat = event.data.lat as number;\n    const lng = event.data.lng as number;\n    \n    if (lat && lng && Math.abs(lat) <= 90 && Math.abs(lng) <= 180) {\n      return {\n        lat,\n        lon: lng,\n        accuracy: 100,\n      };\n    }\n    \n    // Sinon, essayer le reverse geocoding\n    if (event.data.place) {\n      const result = await this.geocoder.geocode(event.data.place as string);\n      if (result) return result;\n    }\n    \n    return undefined;\n  }\n}\n\n// ── Stage 3: Scoring ──\nclass ScoringStage {\n  private scorer: IntelligenceScorer;\n\n  constructor() {\n    this.scorer = new IntelligenceScorer();\n  }\n\n  async process(events: EnrichedEvent[]): Promise<IntelEvent[]> {\n    return events.map(event => ({\n      ...event,\n      score: this.scorer.score(event),\n      correlations: [],\n      anomalies: this.detectAnomalies(event),\n    }));\n  }\n\n  private detectAnomalies(event: EnrichedEvent): IntelEvent['anomalies'] {\n    const anomalies: IntelEvent['anomalies'] = [];\n    \n    // Anomalies spécifiques par type\n    if (event.source_type === 'quake') {\n      const mag = event.data.magnitude as number;\n      if (mag >= 6) {\n        anomalies.push({\n          type: 'major_earthquake',\n          severity: 'CRITICAL',\n          description: `M${mag} earthquake detected`,\n        });\n      }\n      if (event.data.tsunami) {\n        anomalies.push({\n          type: 'tsunami_risk',\n          severity: 'CRITICAL',\n          description: 'Tsunami flag active',\n        });\n      }\n    }\n    \n    if (event.source_type === 'adsb') {\n      const cluster = this.detectMilitaryCluster(event);\n      if (cluster) {\n        anomalies.push({\n          type: 'military_aviation_cluster',\n          severity: 'HIGH',\n          description: `Military aircraft cluster detected`,\n        });\n      }\n    }\n    \n    return anomalies;\n  }\n}\n\n// ── Stage 4: Correlation ──\nclass CorrelationStage {\n  private engine: CorrelationEngine;\n  private contextWindow: number = 3600_000; // 1 heure\n\n  constructor() {\n    this.engine = new CorrelationEngine();\n  }\n\n  async process(events: IntelEvent[], context: IntelEvent[]): Promise<IntelEvent[]> {\n    const correlated: IntelEvent[] = [];\n\n    for (const event of events) {\n      // Filtrer le contexte (même fenêtre temporelle)\n      const eventTime = Date.parse(event.timestamp);\n      const relevantContext = context.filter(ctx => {\n        const ctxTime = Date.parse(ctx.timestamp);\n        return Math.abs(eventTime - ctxTime) < this.contextWindow;\n      });\n\n      // Corréler\n      const correlations = await this.engine.correlate(event, relevantContext);\n      \n      correlated.push({\n        ...event,\n        correlations,\n      });\n    }\n\n    return correlated;\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 10. Plugin System\n\n### 10.1 Plugin Architecture\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                         PLUGIN SYSTEM                                       │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  PLUGIN MANAGER                                                      │   │\n│  │                                                                       │   │\n│  │  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐  ┌──────────┐             │   │\n│  │  │ Registry │  │ Loader   │  │ Sandbox  │  │ Watcher  │             │   │\n│  │  │ (Index)  │──▶│ (Import) │──▶│ (VM)     │──▶│ (FS)     │             │   │\n│  │  └──────────┘  └──────────┘  └──────────┘  └──────────┘             │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  PLUGIN TYPES                                                         │   │\n│  │                                                                       │   │\n│  │  ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐│   │\n│  │  │ Data Source  │ │ Analyzer     │ │ Visualizer   │ │ Exporter     ││   │\n│  │  │ (Seeder)     │ │ (Scorer)     │ │ (Component)  │ │ (Format)     ││   │\n│  │  └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘ └──────────────┘│   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  PLUGIN LIFECYCLE                                                     │   │\n│  │                                                                       │   │\n│  │  DISCOVERED → LOADED → VALIDATED → ACTIVATED → RUNNING → DEACTIVATED │   │\n│  │                    ↓                                                   │   │\n│  │                 ERROR → FAILED                                         │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 10.2 Plugin Manifest\n\n```typescript\n// ── Plugin Manifest Schema ──\ninterface PluginManifest {\n  // Métadonnées\n  name: string;\n  version: string;\n  author: string;\n  description: string;\n  license: string;\n  \n  // UI\n  ui: {\n    icon: string;           // Lucide icon name\n    displayName: string;\n    color: string;          // Hex color\n    category: string;       // 'source' | 'analyzer' | 'visualizer' | 'exporter'\n  };\n  \n  // Capacités\n  capabilities: {\n    permissions: Array<\n      'network' | 'filesystem' | 'process' | 'storage' | 'events'\n    >;\n    hooks: Array<\n      'onEvent' | 'onAnalyze' | 'onRender' | 'onAlert' | 'onStream'\n    >;\n    events: {\n      publishes: string[];\n      subscribes: string[];\n    };\n  };\n  \n  // Dépendances\n  dependencies: {\n    plugins: Array<{ name: string; version: string }>;\n    packages: Array<{ name: string; version: string }>;\n  };\n  \n  // Sécurité\n  security: {\n    sandbox: {\n      enabled: boolean;\n      memoryLimit: number;      // MB\n      networkWhitelist: string[];\n      filesystemWhitelist: string[];\n    };\n    verified: boolean;\n    signature?: string;\n  };\n  \n  // Configuration\n  config: Record<string, {\n    type: 'string' | 'number' | 'boolean' | 'select';\n    default: unknown;\n    description: string;\n    required: boolean;\n    options?: string[];        // Pour type 'select'\n  }>;\n}\n\n// ── Exemple: Plugin Shodan ──\nconst SHODAN_PLUGIN_MANIFEST: PluginManifest = {\n  name: 'shodan-enricher',\n  version: '1.0.0',\n  author: 'Osiris Team',\n  description: 'Enrichit les IPs avec les données Shodan',\n  license: 'MIT',\n  ui: {\n    icon: 'Search',\n    displayName: 'Shodan Enricher',\n    color: '#00FF88',\n    category: 'analyzer',\n  },\n  capabilities: {\n    permissions: ['network', 'storage'],\n    hooks: ['onEvent', 'onAnalyze'],\n    events: {\n      publishes: ['osiris:enriched:shodan'],\n      subscribes: ['osiris:raw:scanner'],\n    },\n  },\n  dependencies: {\n    plugins: [],\n    packages: [{ name: 'shodan-client', version: '^1.0' }],\n  },\n  security: {\n    sandbox: {\n      enabled: true,\n      memoryLimit: 128,\n      networkWhitelist: ['api.shodan.io'],\n      filesystemWhitelist: [],\n    },\n    verified: false,\n  },\n  config: {\n    apiKey: {\n      type: 'string',\n      default: '',\n      description: 'Shodan API Key',\n      required: true,\n    },\n    maxRequestsPerMinute: {\n      type: 'number',\n      default: 10,\n      description: 'Rate limit',\n      required: false,\n    },\n  },\n};\n```\n\n### 10.3 Plugin API\n\n```typescript\n// ── Plugin Context (injecté au chargement) ──\ninterface PluginContext {\n  // Redis client\n  redis: Redis;\n  \n  // Event bus\n  publish(channel: string, message: unknown): Promise<void>;\n  subscribe(channel: string, handler: (message: unknown) => void): Promise<void>;\n  \n  // Storage\n  cache: {\n    get(key: string): Promise<string | null>;\n    set(key: string, value: string, ttl?: number): Promise<void>;\n    del(key: string): Promise<void>;\n  };\n  \n  // Logging\n  log: {\n    info(msg: string, meta?: Record<string, unknown>): void;\n    warn(msg: string, meta?: Record<string, unknown>): void;\n    error(msg: string, meta?: Record<string, unknown>): void;\n    debug(msg: string, meta?: Record<string, unknown>): void;\n  };\n  \n  // Configuration\n  config: Record<string, unknown>;\n  \n  // Métriques\n  metrics: {\n    increment(counter: string, value?: number): void;\n    gauge(name: string, value: number): void;\n    timing(name: string, durationMs: number): void;\n  };\n}\n\n// ── Plugin Base Class ──\nabstract class OsirisPlugin {\n  protected ctx!: PluginContext;\n  protected manifest!: PluginManifest;\n\n  // ── Cycle de vie ──\n  abstract onLoad(ctx: PluginContext): Promise<void>;\n  abstract onUnload(): Promise<void>;\n  \n  // ── Hooks optionnels ──\n  onActivate?(): Promise<void>;\n  onDeactivate?(): Promise<void>;\n  onConfigChange?(config: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<void>;\n  \n  // ── Hooks fonctionnels ──\n  onEvent?(event: IntelEvent): Promise<IntelEvent | null>;\n  onAnalyze?(events: IntelEvent[]): Promise<IntelEvent[]>;\n  onAlert?(alert: IntelEvent): Promise<void>;\n  onStream?(events: IntelEvent[]): AsyncGenerator<IntelEvent>;\n}\n\n// ── Exemple: Plugin Shodan ──\nclass ShodanPlugin extends OsirisPlugin {\n  private client: ShodanClient | null = null;\n\n  async onLoad(ctx: PluginContext): Promise<void> {\n    this.ctx = ctx;\n    \n    const apiKey = ctx.config.apiKey as string;\n    if (!apiKey) {\n      throw new Error('Shodan API key required');\n    }\n    \n    this.client = new ShodanClient(apiKey);\n    ctx.log.info('Shodan plugin loaded');\n  }\n\n  async onUnload(): Promise<void> {\n    this.client = null;\n    this.ctx.log.info('Shodan plugin unloaded');\n  }\n\n  async onEvent(event: IntelEvent): Promise<IntelEvent | null> {\n    // Ne traiter que les événements avec IP\n    const ips = event.ioc?.ips;\n    if (!ips || ips.length === 0) return null;\n    \n    try {\n      const shodanData = await this.client!.search(ips[0]);\n      \n      return {\n        ...event,\n        metadata: {\n          ...event.metadata,\n          tags: [...event.metadata.tags, 'shodan-enriched'],\n        },\n        data: {\n          ...event.data,\n          shodan: shodanData,\n        },\n      };\n    } catch (error) {\n      this.ctx.log.error('Shodan enrichment failed', { error, ip: ips[0] });\n      return null;\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n### 10.4 Sandbox Security\n\n```typescript\n// ── Sandbox VM pour isolation des plugins ──\nclass PluginSandbox {\n  private vm: vm.Script | null = null;\n  private memoryLimit: number;\n  private networkWhitelist: string[];\n\n  constructor(manifest: PluginManifest) {\n    this.memoryLimit = manifest.security.sandbox.memoryLimit;\n    this.networkWhitelist = manifest.security.sandbox.networkWhitelist;\n  }\n\n  async execute<T>(code: string, context: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<T> {\n    // Créer un contexte VM isolé\n    const sandbox = {\n      ...context,\n      console: {\n        log: (...args: unknown[]) => this.safeLog('info', ...args),\n        warn: (...args: unknown[]) => this.safeLog('warn', ...args),\n        error: (...args: unknown[]) => this.safeLog('error', ...args),\n      },\n      fetch: this.createSafeFetch(),\n      setTimeout: this.createSafeTimer(),\n      Math,\n      JSON,\n      Date,\n      Array,\n      Object,\n      String,\n      Number,\n      Boolean,\n      RegExp,\n      Map,\n      Set,\n      Promise,\n    };\n\n    const vmContext = vm.createContext(sandbox);\n    const script = new vm.Script(code);\n    \n    // Exécuter avec limite de mémoire\n    const timeout = setTimeout(() => {\n      throw new Error('Plugin execution timeout');\n    }, 30_000);\n\n    try {\n      const result = await script.runInContext(vmContext, {\n        timeout: 30_000,\n        breakOnSigint: true,\n      });\n      return result as T;\n    } finally {\n      clearTimeout(timeout);\n    }\n  }\n\n  private createSafeFetch(): typeof fetch {\n    return async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init?: RequestInit) => {\n      const url = typeof input === 'string' ? input : input.toString();\n      \n      // Vérifier whitelist\n      const allowed = this.networkWhitelist.some(pattern => url.includes(pattern));\n      if (!allowed) {\n        throw new Error(`Network access to ${url} not allowed`);\n      }\n      \n      return fetch(input, init);\n    };\n  }\n\n  private createSafeTimer() {\n    return (fn: Function, ms: number) => {\n      const maxMs = Math.min(ms, 10_000); // Max 10s\n      return setTimeout(fn, maxMs);\n    };\n  }\n\n  private safeLog(level: string, ...args: unknown[]) {\n    const message = args.map(a => \n      typeof a === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(a, null, 2) : String(a)\n    ).join(' ');\n    \n    // Limiter la taille des logs\n    const truncated = message.length > 1000 ? message.slice(0, 1000) + '...' : message;\n    \n    console[level as keyof Console](`[Plugin] ${truncated}`);\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 11. Security Architecture\n\n### 11.1 Security Layers\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                         SECURITY ARCHITECTURE                               │\n│                                                                              │\n│  LAYER 1: NETWORK                                                           │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  ● Docker network isolation (osiris-net)                             │   │\n│  │  ● CORS whitelist                                                    │   │\n│  │  ● Rate limiting (Redis sliding window)                              │   │\n│  │  ● IP filtering (allow/deny lists)                                   │   │\n│  │  ● TLS/SSL termination (reverse proxy)                               │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  LAYER 2: AUTHENTICATION                                                     │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  ● JWT (RS256) with short expiry (15 min)                            │   │\n│  │  ● Refresh tokens (7 days)                                           │   │\n│  │  ● API Keys for programmatic access                                  │   │\n│  │  ● OAuth2 providers (GitHub, Google)                                 │   │\n│  │  ● 2FA (TOTP) for admin accounts                                     │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  LAYER 3: AUTHORIZATION                                                      │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  ● RBAC (Role-Based Access Control)                                  │   │\n│  │  ● Roles: admin, analyst, viewer, api                                │   │\n│  │  ● Permissions: modules, feeds, export, admin                        │   │\n│  │  ● Resource-level access control                                     │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  LAYER 4: APPLICATION                                                       │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  ● SSRF Guard (block private IPs)                                    │   │\n│  │  ● Input validation (Zod schemas)                                    │   │\n│  │  ● SQL/NoSQL injection prevention                                    │   │\n│  │  ● XSS prevention (CSP headers)                                      │   │\n│  │  ● CSRF tokens                                                       │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  LAYER 5: DATA                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  ● Encryption at rest (AES-256)                                      │   │\n│  │  ● Encryption in transit (TLS 1.3)                                   │   │\n│  │  ● Secrets management (.env + vault)                                 │   │\n│  │  ● Data retention policies                                           │   │\n│  │  ● Audit logging                                                     │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 11.2 JWT Implementation\n\n```typescript\n// ── JWT Token Structure ──\ninterface JWTPayload {\n  sub: string;           // User ID\n  role: 'admin' | 'analyst' | 'viewer' | 'api';\n  permissions: string[];\n  iat: number;           // Issued at\n  exp: number;           // Expiration\n  jti: string;           // JWT ID (for revocation)\n}\n\n// ── JWT Middleware ──\nasync function jwtMiddleware(req: NextRequest): Promise<NextResponse | null> {\n  const [REDACTED:ENV_VAR]  if (!token) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const payload = await verifyJWT(token);\n    \n    // Vérifier si le token n'est pas révoqué\n    const isRevoked = await redis.get(`revoked:${payload.jti}`);\n    if (isRevoked) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Token revoked' }, { status: 401 });\n    }\n    \n    // Attacher le payload à la requête\n    (req as any).user = payload;\n    return null; // Continue\n  } catch (error) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Invalid token' }, { status: 401 });\n  }\n}\n\n// ── RBAC Check ──\nfunction checkPermission(user: JWTPayload, requiredPermission: string): boolean {\n  if (user.role === 'admin') return true;\n  return user.permissions.includes(requiredPermission);\n}\n```\n\n### 11.3 SSRF Guard\n\n```typescript\n// ── SSRF Protection ──\nclass SSRFGuard {\n  private blockedRanges: string[] = [\n    '127.0.0.0/8',      // Localhost\n    '10.0.0.0/8',       // Private\n    '172.16.0.0/12',    // Private\n    '192.168.0.0/16',   // Private\n    '169.254.0.0/16',   // Link-local\n    '0.0.0.0/8',        // Invalid\n    '::1/128',          // IPv6 localhost\n    'fc00::/7',         // IPv6 private\n    'fe80::/10',        // IPv6 link-local\n  ];\n\n  async validate(url: string): Promise<boolean> {\n    try {\n      const parsed = new URL(url);\n      const hostname = parsed.hostname;\n      \n      // Vérifier si c'est une IP\n      const ip = await this.resolveHostname(hostname);\n      if (!ip) return true; // Hostname non résolu = potentiellement valide\n      \n      // Vérifier si l'IP est dans les ranges bloqués\n      for (const range of this.blockedRanges) {\n        if (this.ipInRange(ip, range)) {\n          return false;\n        }\n      }\n      \n      return true;\n    } catch {\n      return false;\n    }\n  }\n\n  private async resolveHostname(hostname: string): Promise<string | null> {\n    // Utiliser DNS pour résoudre le hostname\n    try {\n      const addresses = await dns.promises.resolve4(hostname);\n      return addresses[0] || null;\n    } catch {\n      return null;\n    }\n  }\n\n  private ipInRange(ip: string, range: string): boolean {\n    // Vérification CIDR simplifiée\n    const [base, bits] = range.split('/');\n    const mask = ~(2 ** (32 - parseInt(bits)) - 1);\n    const ipNum = this.ipToNumber(ip);\n    const baseNum = this.ipToNumber(base);\n    return (ipNum & mask) === (baseNum & mask);\n  }\n\n  private ipToNumber(ip: string): number {\n    return ip.split('.').reduce((acc, octet) => (acc << 8) + parseInt(octet), 0) >>> 0;\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 12. Scalability Patterns\n\n### 12.1 Horizontal Scaling\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                         SCALABILITY PATTERNS                                │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  1. CONSUMER GROUP SCALING                                           │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Redis Stream ──▶ Consumer Group ──▶ Consumer 1 (partition 1)        │   │\n│  │                    (osiris:enrichers)  ├── Consumer 2 (partition 2)  │   │\n│  │                                        └── Consumer 3 (partition 3)  │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Chaque message est livré à UN seul consumer du groupe               │   │\n│  │  Scaling: ajouter des consumers = plus de parallélisme               │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  2. SEEDER SHARDING                                                  │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Source API ──▶ Seeder 1 (région EU) ──▶ osiris:raw:{source}:eu     │   │\n│  │              ──▶ Seeder 2 (région US) ──▶ osiris:raw:{source}:us    │   │\n│  │              ──▶ Seeder 3 (région AS) ──▶ osiris:raw:{source}:as    │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Scaling: sharder par région géographique                            │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  3. CACHE SHARDING                                                   │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Redis Cluster ──▶ Node 1 (slots 0-5460)   ── Cache clés {a-m}*     │   │\n│  │                ──▶ Node 2 (slots 5461-10922) ── Cache clés {n-z}*   │   │\n│  │                ──▶ Node 3 (slots 10923-16383) ── Cache clés {0-9}*  │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Scaling: ajouter des nœuds Redis Cluster                            │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  4. READ REPLICAS                                                    │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Neo4j Primary ──▶ Neo4j Replica 1 (lectures)                       │   │\n│  │                ──▶ Neo4j Replica 2 (lectures)                       │   │\n│  │                ──▶ Neo4j Replica 3 (lectures)                       │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  Scaling: ajouter des réplicas de lecture                            │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 12.2 Performance Targets\n\n```typescript\n// ── Performance Budget ──\nconst PERFORMANCE_BUDGET = {\n  // Frontend\n  frontend: {\n    firstPaint: 500,           // ms\n    firstContentfulPaint: 1000, // ms\n    timeToInteractive: 2000,    // ms\n    splashDuration: 2500,       // ms\n    mapFrameRate: 18,           // fps (limit pour économie)\n    maxFeatures: 2000,          // features sur la carte\n  },\n  \n  // API\n  api: {\n    p50Latency: 100,            // ms\n    p95Latency: 500,            // ms\n    p99Latency: 2000,           // ms\n    throughput: 1000,           // req/s\n  },\n  \n  // Event Processing\n  eventProcessing: {\n    ingestionLatency: 100,      // ms (source → stream)\n    enrichmentLatency: 50,      // ms\n    scoringLatency: 20,         // ms\n    correlationLatency: 100,    // ms\n    totalPipelineLatency: 500,  // ms (source → dashboard)\n    throughput: 10000,          // events/s\n  },\n  \n  // WebSocket\n  websocket: {\n    connectionLatency: 50,      // ms\n    messageLatency: 100,        // ms\n    maxConnections: 10000,      // concurrent\n    reconnectDelay: 750,        // ms (initial)\n  },\n  \n  // Storage\n  storage: {\n    redisHitRate: 0.95,         // 95% cache hits\n    neo4jQueryTime: 100,        // ms\n    meilisearchQueryTime: 50,   // ms\n  },\n};\n```\n\n### 12.3 Resource Estimation\n\n```typescript\n// ── Estimation des ressources pour 10K events/s ──\nconst RESOURCE_ESTIMATION = {\n  redis: {\n    instances: 3,               // Cluster mode\n    memory: '4GB',              // Par instance\n    cpu: '4 cores',\n    storage: '100GB SSD',       // Persistence AOF\n  },\n  \n  neo4j: {\n    instances: 2,               // 1 primary + 1 replica\n    memory: '8GB',\n    cpu: '4 cores',\n    storage: '50GB SSD',\n  },\n  \n  apiGateway: {\n    instances: 2,\n    memory: '1GB',\n    cpu: '2 cores',\n  },\n  \n  seeders: {\n    instances: 5,               // 5 seeder processes\n    memory: '512MB',\n    cpu: '1 core',\n  },\n  \n  processing: {\n    instances: 3,               // Consumer group workers\n    memory: '1GB',\n    cpu: '2 cores',\n  },\n  \n  frontend: {\n    instances: 2,\n    memory: '512MB',\n    cpu: '2 cores',\n  },\n  \n  total: {\n    memory: '~24GB',\n    cpu: '~24 cores',\n    storage: '~200GB SSD',\n  },\n};\n```\n\n---\n\n## Annexe A : Diagramme de Déploiement\n\n```mermaid\ngraph TD\n Reverse Proxy]\n        Nginx -->|/api/*| Gateway\n        Nginx -->|/*| Frontend\n        Nginx -->|/ws/*| Gateway\n        Nginx -->|:9090| Prometheus\n        Nginx -->|:3000| Grafana\n        \n        Gateway --> Auth\n        Gateway --> Redis\n        Gateway --> AI\n        Gateway --> GEOINT\n        Gateway --> Threat\n        Gateway --> Graph\n        Gateway --> Search\n        \n RSS\n```\n\n---\n\n## Annexe B : Glossaire Technique\n\n| Terme | Définition |\n|-------|------------|\n| **Consumer Group** | Groupe de consommateurs Redis Stream qui se partagent les messages |\n| **Stream** | Log append-only Redis, structure de données pour l'event sourcing |\n| **Pub/Sub** | Pattern de messagerie publish/subscribe Redis |\n| **TTL** | Time-To-Live, durée de vie d'une clé dans le cache |\n| **CIDR** | Classless Inter-Domain Routing, notation pour les plages IP |\n| **SSRF** | Server-Side Request Forgery, attaque ciblant les requêtes serveur |\n| **RBAC** | Role-Based Access Control, contrôle d'accès par rôles |\n| **JWT** | JSON Web Token, token d'authentification |\n| **TLE** | Two-Line Element, format de données orbitales satellites |\n| **ADS-B** | Automatic Dependent Surveillance-Broadcast, tracking aérien |\n| **AIS** | Automatic Identification System, tracking maritime |\n| **GDELT** | Global Database of Events, Language, and Tone |\n| **KEV** | Known Exploited Vulnerabilities, catalogue CISA |\n| **FIRMS** | Fire Information for Resource Management System, NASA |\n| **IOC** | Indicator of Compromise, indicateur de compromission |\n| **CVE** | Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures |\n| **Neo4j** | Base de données graphe native |\n| **Meilisearch** | Moteur de recherche full-text open-source |\n| **MinIO** | Stockage objet compatible S3 |\n| **MapLibre GL** | Bibliothèque cartographique open-source WebGL |\n\n---\n\n*Document d'architecture technique généré pour Osiris-Lab v4.2 — 18 juin ## ✅ Architecture Technique Complète d'Osiris-Lab

Deux documents d'architecture ont été créés :

### 📄 ARCHITECTURE.md (1170 lignes)
Document d'architecture générale couvrant :
- Vue d'ensemble, principes et stack technologique
- Architecture logicielle 5 couches + diagramme Docker
- Frontend dashboard (design system, états, responsive)
- Backend modulaire (35+ endpoints, 8 modules backend)
- Event-Driven Architecture (Redis Streams, Pub/Sub, Core Brain)
- Graph Intelligence System (10 entités, 8 relations, moteur de scoring)
- GEOINT Engine (9 couches, satellite tracking)
- Threat Intelligence Engine (10 sources, pipeline d'analyse)
- AI Analysis Layer (Gemini 2.0 Flash, rotation 8 clés API)
- Plugin Engine + Digital Twin
- Flux de données complets (18 sources, optimisations réseau)
- Roadmap 4 phases (v4.0 → v6.0)

### ⚙️ TECHNICAL_ARCHITECTURE.md (1500+ lignes)
Document d'architecture technique détaillée couvrant :

| Section | Détails |
|---------|---------|
| **1. Event-Driven Architecture** | Topologie Redis Streams (6 streams, 5 consumer groups, 3 pub/sub channels), 3 schémas de message (Raw → Enriched → Intel), implémentation StreamConsumer, pipeline 5 stages |
| **2. Modular Backend Services** | Architecture service mesh, 5 engines spécifiés (AI, GEOINT, Threat, Graph, Search) avec interfaces TypeScript complètes, service discovery + health checks |
| **3. Real-Time Data Processing** | WebSocket architecture complète, protocole bidirectionnel (7 messages serveur, 4 messages client), polling strategy avec layer-aware fetching, EventDeduplicator LRU |
| **4. Intelligence Graph Core** | Schéma Neo4j complet (contraintes, index, 12 types de nœuds, 9 types de relations, 5 requêtes typiques), EntityResolver pipeline (6 matchers), CorrelationEngine (spatial 250km, temporel 1h, causal, attributs), IntelligenceScorer (importance/risque/confiance) |
| **5. Frontend Dashboard** | Arbre de composants complet (50+ composants), DashboardState typé, useDashboardState hook, MAP_PERFORMANCE_CONFIG, GeocodeCache LRU |
| **6. Inter-Module Communication** | 4 patterns (REST synchrone, Redis async, WebSocket temps réel, Pub/Sub événementiel), ModuleContract interface, Circuit Breaker pattern |
| **7. Global Data Flow** | End-to-end pipeline (sources → ingestion → processing → stockage → delivery), flows par type (ADS-B, Earthquake avec formats), diagramme Mermaid |
| **8. Storage Architecture** | 4 couches (Redis, Neo4j, Meilisearch, MinIO), Redis key naming convention, 10 TTL configs, 5 queries Neo4j, Meilisearch configuration |
| **9. Ingestion Pipeline** | Seeder architecture (Template Method pattern), 30+ seeders listés, EarthquakeSeeder implémenté, 4 processing stages (Validation, Enrichment, Scoring, Correlation) avec code complet |
| **10. Plugin System** | PluginManager (Registry/Loader/Sandbox/Watcher), 4 types de plugins, manifest complet (20+ champs), PluginContext injecté, ShodanPlugin exemple, Sandbox VM sécurisée |
| **11. Security Architecture** | 5 couches (Network, Auth, Authorization, Application, Data), JWT implémentation, RBAC, SSRF Guard avec validation CIDR |
| **12. Scalability Patterns** | 4 patterns (Consumer Group, Seeder Sharding, Cache Sharding, Read Replicas), Performance Budget (15 métriques), Resource Estimation (24GB RAM, 24 cores, 200GB SSD) |

### Flux de Données Clés Documentés
1. **Ingestion** : 30+ seeders → Redis Streams → Consumer Groups (5 pipelines parallèles)
2. **Traitement** : Validation → Enrichissement Geo/IOC → Scoring (importance×0.4 + risque×0.35 + confiance×0.25) → Corrélation spatiale/temporelle/causale → Alerting
3. **Stockage** : Redis (cache 5-60min TTL) → Neo4j (graphe persistant) → Meilisearch (full-text) → MinIO (objets)
4. **Livraison** : WebSocket protocole binaire (flush 250ms, max 2000 events, reconnect exponentiel)HAS_CHANGES

Indexing\n\n```typescript\n// ── H3 Hexagonal Indexing pour clustering spatial ──\nimport h3 from 'h3-js';\n\nclass H3SpatialIndex {\n  private resolution: number = 8; // Résolution H3 (hexagone ~0.7km²)\n\n  // Indexer une entité géographique\n  indexEntity(entity: GeoEntity): string {\n    return h3.latLngToCell(entity.lat, entity.lng, this.resolution);\n  }\n\n  // Clustering par densité H3\n  clusterByDensity(entities: GeoEntity[], minDensity: number = 5): Cluster[] {\n    const hexCounts = new Map<string, { entities: GeoEntity[]; center: Coord }>();\n\n    for (const entity of entities) {\n      const hex = this.indexEntity(entity);\n      if (!hexCounts.has(hex)) {\n        const center = h3.cellToLatLng(hex);\n        hexCounts.set(hex, { entities: [], center: { lat: center[0], lng: center[1] } });\n      }\n      hexCounts.get(hex)!.entities.push(entity);\n    }\n\n    // Filtrer par densité minimale\n    const clusters: Cluster[] = [];\n    for (const [hex, data] of hexCounts) {\n      if (data.entities.length >= minDensity) {\n        // Fusionner avec les hexagones voisins\n        const neighbors = h3.gridDisk(hex, 1);\n        let allEntities = [...data.entities];\n        \n        for (const neighbor of neighbors) {\n          const neighborData = hexCounts.get(neighbor);\n          if (neighborData && neighbor !== hex) {\n            allEntities = [...allEntities, ...neighborData.entities];\n            hexCounts.delete(neighbor); // Éviter double comptage\n          }\n        }\n\n        clusters.push({\n          id: hex,\n          center: data.center,\n          entities: allEntities,\n          density: allEntities.length,\n          radius: this.estimateRadius(allEntities),\n          hexagons: [hex, ...neighbors],\n        });\n      }\n    }\n\n    return clusters.sort((a, b) => b.density - a.density);\n  }\n\n  // Recherche par rayon\n  queryRadius(center: Coord, radiusKm: number): string[] {\n    return h3.gridDisk(h3.latLngToCell(center.lat, center.lng, this.resolution), \n                       Math.ceil(radiusKm / 0.7));\n  }\n\n  // Estimation du rayon du cluster\n  private estimateRadius(entities: GeoEntity[]): number {\n    if (entities.length < 2) return 0;\n    let maxDist = 0;\n    for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(entities.length, 50); i++) {\n      for (let j = i + 1; j < Math.min(entities.length, 50); j++) {\n        const dist = this.haversine(entities[i].lat, entities[i].lng, \n                                     entities[j].lat, entities[j].lng);\n        maxDist = Math.max(maxDist, dist);\n      }\n    }\n    return maxDist / 2;\n  }\n\n  private haversine(lat1: number, lon1: number, lat2: number, lon2: number): number {\n    const R = 6371;\n    const dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180;\n    const dLon = (lon2 - lon1) * Math.PI / 180;\n    const a = Math.sin(dLat/2)**2 + Math.cos(lat1 * Math.PI/180) * Math.cos(lat2 * Math.PI/180) * Math.sin(dLon/2)**2;\n    return 2 * R * Math.asin(Math.sqrt(a));\n  }\n}\n\ninterface Cluster {\n  id: string;\n  center: Coord;\n  entities: GeoEntity[];\n  density: number;\n  radius: number;\n  hexagons: string[];\n}\n\ninterface Coord { lat: number; lng: number; }\ninterface GeoEntity { lat: number; lng: number; [key: string]: unknown; }\n```\n\n### 2.3 Trajectory Analyzer\n\n```typescript\n// ── Analyseur de trajectoires ──\nclass TrajectoryAnalyzer {\n  private history: Map<string, PositionRecord[]> = new Map();\n  private maxHistoryMinutes: number = 60;\n\n  // Enregistrer une position\n  recordPosition(entityId: string, pos: PositionRecord): void {\n    if (!this.history.has(entityId)) {\n      this.history.set(entityId, []);\n    }\n    \n    const trajectory = this.history.get(entityId)!;\n    trajectory.push(pos);\n    \n    // Nettoyage (garder X minutes)\n    const cutoff = Date.now() - this.maxHistoryMinutes * 60 * 1000;\n    while (trajectory.length > 0 && trajectory[0].timestamp < cutoff) {\n      trajectory.shift();\n    }\n    \n    // Limiter à 1000 points max\n    if (trajectory.length > 1000) {\n      trajectory.splice(0, trajectory.length - 1000);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Analyser une trajectoire pour anomalies\n  analyzeTrajectory(entityId: string): TrajectoryAnomaly[] {\n    const trajectory = this.history.get(entityId);\n    if (!trajectory || trajectory.length < 3) return [];\n\n    const anomalies: TrajectoryAnomaly[] = [];\n\n    // 1. Détection de loitering (déplacement anormalement lent)\n    anomalies.push(...this.detectLoitering(trajectory, entityId));\n\n    // 2. Détection de changement de cap brusque\n    anomalies.push(...this.detectSharpTurn(trajectory, entityId));\n\n    // 3. Détection de vitesse anormale\n    anomalies.push(...this.detectSpeedAnomaly(trajectory, entityId));\n\n    // 4. Détection d'altitude anormale (aviation)\n    anomalies.push(...this.detectAltitudeAnomaly(trajectory, entityId));\n\n    return anomalies;\n  }\n\n  private detectLoitering(trajectory: PositionRecord[], entityId: string): TrajectoryAnomaly[] {\n    const anomalies: TrajectoryAnomaly[] = [];\n    const recentPoints = trajectory.slice(-10); // 10 derniers points\n    \n    if (recentPoints.length < 5) return anomalies;\n    \n    let totalDistance = 0;\n    for (let i = 1; i < recentPoints.length; i++) {\n      totalDistance += this.haversine(\n        recentPoints[i-1].lat, recentPoints[i-1].lng,\n        recentPoints[i].lat, recentPoints[i].lng\n      );\n    }\n    \n    const avgSpeed = totalDistance / (recentPoints.length - 1); // km par intervalle\n    \n    // Si vitesse moyenne < 5 km/h (hors contexte maritime lent) et durée > 10 min\n    const timeSpan = (recentPoints[recentPoints.length-1].timestamp - recentPoints[0].timestamp) / 60000;\n    if (avgSpeed < 0.5 && timeSpan > 10) {\n      anomalies.push({\n        entityId,\n        type: 'LOITERING',\n        severity: avgSpeed < 0.1 ? 'HIGH' : 'MEDIUM',\n        description: `Loitering detected: ${avgSpeed.toFixed(2)} km/interval over ${timeSpan.toFixed(0)} min`,\n        confidence: Math.min(90, 50 + timeSpan * 2),\n        position: recentPoints[recentPoints.length - 1],\n        timestamp: Date.now(),\n      });\n    }\n    \n    return anomalies;\n  }\n\n  private detectSharpTurn(trajectory: PositionRecord[], entityId: string): TrajectoryAnomaly[] {\n    const anomalies: TrajectoryAnomaly[] = [];\n    \n    if (trajectory.length < 3) return anomalies;\n    \n    const last3 = trajectory.slice(-3);\n    const heading1 = this.calculateHeading(last3[0], last3[1]);\n    const heading2 = this.calculateHeading(last3[1], last3[2]);\n    \n    if (heading1 === null || heading2 === null) return anomalies;\n    \n    const change = Math.abs(heading2 - heading1);\n    const normalizedChange = change > 180 ? 360 - change : change;\n    \n    if (normalizedChange > 120) {\n      anomalies.push({\n        entityId,\n        type: 'SHARP_TURN',\n        severity: normalizedChange > 160 ? 'HIGH' : 'MEDIUM',\n        description: `Sharp turn detected: ${normalizedChange.toFixed(0)}° change`,\n        confidence: 75,\n        position: last3[2],\n        timestamp: Date.now(),\n      });\n    }\n    \n    return anomalies;\n  }\n\n  private detectSpeedAnomaly(trajectory: PositionRecord[], entityId: string): TrajectoryAnomaly[] {\n    const anomalies: TrajectoryAnomaly[] = [];\n    \n    if (trajectory.length < 5) return anomalies;\n    \n    const speeds: number[] = [];\n    for (let i = 1; i < trajectory.length; i++) {\n      const dist = this.haversine(\n        trajectory[i-1].lat, trajectory[i-1].lng,\n        trajectory[i].lat, trajectory[i].lng\n      );\n      const time = (trajectory[i].timestamp - trajectory[i-1].timestamp) / 3600; // heures\n      if (time > 0) {\n        speeds.push(dist / time); // km/h\n      }\n    }\n    \n    if (speeds.length < 3) return anomalies;\n    \n    // Z-score pour détecter les outliers\n    const mean = speeds.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / speeds.length;\n    const variance = speeds.reduce((a, b) => a + (b - mean) ** 2, 0) / speeds.length;\n    const stdDev = Math.sqrt(variance);\n    \n    const lastSpeed = speeds[speeds.length - 1];\n    const zScore = stdDev > 0 ? Math.abs(lastSpeed - mean) / stdDev : 0;\n    \n    if (zScore > 2.5) {\n      anomalies.push({\n        entityId,\n        type: 'SPEED_ANOMALY',\n        severity: zScore > 4 ? 'HIGH' : 'MEDIUM',\n        description: `Speed anomaly: ${lastSpeed.toFixed(0)} km/h (z-score: ${zScore.toFixed(2)})`,\n        confidence: Math.min(85, zScore * 20),\n        position: trajectory[trajectory.length - 1],\n        timestamp: Date.now(),\n      });\n    }\n    \n    return anomalies;\n  }\n\n  private detectAltitudeAnomaly(trajectory: PositionRecord[], entityId: string): TrajectoryAnomaly[] {\n    const anomalies: TrajectoryAnomaly[] = [];\n    \n    // Pour les aéronefs, détecter les changements d'altitude rapides\n    const altitudes = trajectory.filter(p => p.altitude !== undefined);\n    if (altitudes.length < 3) return anomalies;\n    \n    const last3 = altitudes.slice(-3);\n    const changeRate1 = (last3[1].altitude! - last3[0].altitude!) / \n                        ((last3[1].timestamp - last3[0].timestamp) / 1000);\n    const changeRate2 = (last3[2].altitude! - last3[1].altitude!) / \n                        ((last3[2].timestamp - last3[1].timestamp) / 1000);\n    \n    // Descente rapide > 30 m/s (6 000 ft/min)\n    if (changeRate2 < -30 && changeRate1 < -20) {\n      anomalies.push({\n        entityId,\n        type: 'RAPID_DESCENT',\n        severity: changeRate2 < -50 ? 'CRITICAL' : 'HIGH',\n        description: `Rapid descent: ${Math.abs(changeRate2).toFixed(0)} m/s`,\n        confidence: 80,\n        position: last3[2],\n        timestamp: Date.now(),\n      });\n    }\n    \n    return anomalies;\n  }\n\n  private calculateHeading(from: PositionRecord, to: PositionRecord): number | null {\n    const dLng = (to.lng - from.lng) * Math.PI / 180;\n    const y = Math.sin(dLng) * Math.cos(to.lat * Math.PI / 180);\n    const x = Math.cos(from.lat * Math.PI / 180) * Math.sin(to.lat * Math.PI / 180) -\n              Math.sin(from.lat * Math.PI / 180) * Math.cos(to.lat * Math.PI / 180) * Math.cos(dLng);\n    const heading = Math.atan2(y, x) * 180 / Math.PI;\n    return (heading + 360) % 360;\n  }\n\n  private haversine(lat1: number, lon1: number, lat2: number, lon2: number): number {\n    const R = 6371;\n    const dLat = (lat2 - lat1) * Math.PI / 180;\n    const dLon = (lon2 - lon1) * Math.PI / 180;\n    const a = Math.sin(dLat/2)**2 + Math.cos(lat1 * Math.PI/180) * Math.cos(lat2 * Math.PI/180) * Math.sin(dLon/2)**2;\n    return 2 * R * Math.asin(Math.sqrt(a));\n  }\n}\n\ninterface PositionRecord {\n  lat: number;\n  lng: number;\n  altitude?: number;\n  speed?: number;\n  heading?: number;\n  timestamp: number;\n  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface TrajectoryAnomaly {\n  entityId: string;\n  type: 'LOITERING' | 'SHARP_TURN' | 'SPEED_ANOMALY' | 'RAPID_DESCENT' | 'GEO_FENCE';\n  severity: 'LOW' | 'MEDIUM' | 'HIGH' | 'CRITICAL';\n  description: string;\n  confidence: number;\n  position: PositionRecord;\n  timestamp: number;\n}\n```\n\n### 2.4 Geo-Fence Engine\n\n```typescript\n// ── Moteur de zones géographiques ──\nclass GeoFenceEngine {\n  private fences: GeoFence[] = [];\n  private alertHistory: Map<string, number> = new Map(); // Cooldown\n\n  constructor() {\n    this.loadDefaultFences();\n  }\n\n  private loadDefaultFences() {\n    // Zones militaires / zones interdites\n    this.fences.push({\n      id: 'ukraine_conflict_zone',\n      name: 'Ukraine Conflict Zone',\n      type: 'CONFLICT_ZONE',\n      polygon: this.loadConflictZonePolygon(),\n      severity: 'CRITICAL',\n      alertOnEntry: true,\n      alertOnExit: false,\n    });\n\n    // Zones maritimes sensibles\n    this.fences.push({\n      id: 'strait_of_hormuz',\n      name: 'Strait of Hormuz - High Risk',\n      type: 'MARITIME_CHOKEPOINT',\n      polygon: this.loadChokepointPolygon('hormuz'),\n      severity: 'HIGH',\n      alertOnEntry: true,\n      alertOnExit: false,\n    });\n\n    // Espaces aériens restreints\n    this.fences.push({\n      id: 'airspace_restricted',\n      name: 'Restricted Airspace Zones',\n      type: 'RESTRICTED_AIRSPACE',\n      polygons: this.loadRestrictedAirspaces(),\n      severity: 'HIGH',\n      alertOnEntry: true,\n      alertOnExit: false,\n    });\n  }\n\n  // Vérifier si une position est dans une zone\n  checkPosition(entity: TrackedEntity): GeoFenceAlert[] {\n    const alerts: GeoFenceAlert[] = [];\n    \n    for (const fence of this.fences) {\n      const isInside = this.pointInPolygon(\n        { lat: entity.lat, lng: entity.lng },\n        fence.polygon\n      );\n\n      if (isInside && fence.alertOnEntry) {\n        // Vérifier cooldown (éviter spam)\n        const key = `${fence.id}:${entity.id}`;\n        const lastAlert = this.alertHistory.get(key) || 0;\n        if (Date.now() - lastAlert > 300_000) { // 5 min cooldown\n          alerts.push({\n            fenceId: fence.id,\n            fenceName: fence.name,\n            entityId: entity.id,\n            entityType: entity.type,\n            severity: fence.severity,\n            description: `${entity.label} entered ${fence.name}`,\n            position: { lat: entity.lat, lng: entity.lng },\n            timestamp: Date.now(),\n          });\n          this.alertHistory.set(key, Date.now());\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    return alerts;\n  }\n\n  // Algorithme de point dans polygone (Ray-Casting)\n  private pointInPolygon(point: Coord, polygon: Coord[][]): boolean {\n    let inside = false;\n    for (const ring of polygon) {\n      for (let i = 0, j = ring.length - 1; i < ring.length; j = i++) {\n        const xi = ring[i].lng, yi = ring[i].lat;\n        const xj = ring[j].lng, yj = ring[j].lat;\n        \n        if ((yi > point.lat) !== (yj > point.lat) &&\n            point.lng < (xj - xi) * (point.lat - yi) / (yj - yi) + xi) {\n          inside = !inside;\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    return inside;\n  }\n}\n\ninterface GeoFence {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  type: 'CONFLICT_ZONE' | 'MARITIME_CHOKEPOINT' | 'RESTRICTED_AIRSPACE' | 'SANCTIONED_COUNTRY';\n  polygon: Coord[][];\n  polygons?: Coord[][][];\n  severity: 'MEDIUM' | 'HIGH' | 'CRITICAL';\n  alertOnEntry: boolean;\n  alertOnExit: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface GeoFenceAlert {\n  fenceId: string;\n  fenceName: string;\n  entityId: string;\n  entityType: string;\n  severity: string;\n  description: string;\n  position: Coord;\n  timestamp: number;\n}\n\ninterface TrackedEntity {\n  id: string;\n  type: 'aircraft' | 'vessel' | 'vehicle' | 'person';\n  label: string;\n  lat: number;\n  lng: number;\n  speed?: number;\n  heading?: number;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 3. Threat Intelligence Engine\n\n### 3.1 Architecture Interne\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                    THREAT INTELLIGENCE ENGINE (:5003)                       │\n│                                                                              │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  COUCHE INGESTION MENACES                                            │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐ │  │  abuse.ch)   │  │ (GDELT, ACLED)│  │  Social)     │               │   │\n│  │  └──────┬───────┘  └──────┬───────┘  └──────┬───────┘               │   │\n│  │         │                 │  │  │                    THREAT NORMALIZER                          │   │   │\n│  │  │  (Normalise en ThreatEvent unifié)                           │   │   │\n│  │  │  COUCHE ENRICHISSEMENT                                              │   │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  ┌──────────────────┐ │   │   │\n│  │  │  les IOC, CVE)   │  │ (Pays, région,   │  │ (Patterns        │   │   │\n│  │  │                  │  │  secteur)         │  │  récurrents)     │   │   │\n│  │  └────────┬─────────┘ severity, geo, iocs, context, score }          │   │   │\n│  │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │   │\n│ │\n│  │                                                                      │   │\n│  │  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │   │\n│  │  │  GEO-THREAT CORRELATOR (séismes, incendies, conflits)                │   │   │\n│  │  │                                                               │   │   │\n│  │  │  Logique: d'entité compatible (avion ⇢ conflit, navire ⇢      │   │   │\n│  │  │  │   chokepoint, satellite ⇢ événement)                      │   │   │\n│  │  │  └── Score de corrélation composite                           │   │   │\n│  │  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│ ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │  COUCHE SCORING & ALERTE                                            │   │\n│  │
