│  │ (0-100)          │──▶│ Calculator       │──▶│ (WebSocket,      │   │   │\n│  │  │                  │   │ (Urgence ×       │   │  Webhook, Log)   │   │   │\n│  │  │                  │   │  Impact × Conf)  │   │                  │   │   │\n│  │  └──────────────────┘  └──────────────────┘  └──────────────────┘   │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 3.2 Geo-Threat Correlator\n\n```typescript\n// ── Corrélateur Géo-Menace ──\nclass GeoThreatCorrelator {\n  private spatialThresholds = {\n    aircraft_conflict: 200,      // km\n    vessel_chokepoint: 50,       // km\n    satellite_event: 500,        // km\n    infrastructure_quake: 250,   // km\n    cyber_country: 0,            // Même pays\n    malware_region: 500,         // km (approximatif)\n  };\n\n  async correlate(\n    geoEntities: TrackedEntity[],\n    threats: ThreatEvent[]\n  ): Promise<GeoThreatCorrelation[]> {\n    const correlations: GeoThreatCorrelation[] = [];\n\n    for (const entity of geoEntities) {\n      for (const threat of threats) {\n        const correlation = await this.analyzePair(entity, threat);\n        if (correlation) {\n          correlations.push(correlation);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Trier par score et retourner top 100\n    return correlations\n      .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score)\n      .slice(0, 100);\n  }\n\n  private async analyzePair(\n    entity: TrackedEntity,\n    threat: ThreatEvent\n  ): Promise<GeoThreatCorrelation | null> {\n    // 1. Vérifier compatibilité de type\n    const relationType = this.getRelationType(entity.type, threat.type);\n    if (!relationType) return null;\n\n    // 2. Vérifier proximité spatiale\n    const distance = this.calculateDistance(\n      { lat: entity.lat, lng: entity.lng },\n      { lat: threat.lat, lng: threat.lng }\n    );\n    \n    const threshold = this.spatialThresholds[relationType as keyof typeof this.spatialThresholds];\n    if (distance > threshold) return null;\n\n    // 3. Vérifier fenêtre temporelle\n    const timeDiff = Math.abs(entity.timestamp - threat.timestamp) / 3600000; // heures\n    if (timeDiff > this.getTimeWindow(relationType)) return null;\n\n    // 4. Calculer score de corrélation\n    const score = this.calculateCorrelationScore(entity, threat, distance, timeDiff);\n\n    if (score < 30) return null; // Seuil minimum\n\n    return {\n      id: `corr:${entity.id}:${threat.id}:${Date.now()}`,\n      entityId: entity.id,\n      entityType: entity.type,\n      entityLabel: entity.label,\n      threatId: threat.id,\n      threatType: threat.type,\n      threatLabel: threat.title,\n      relationType,\n      distance,\n      timeDiffHours: timeDiff,\n      score,\n      severity: this.determineSeverity(score),\n      description: this.generateDescription(entity, threat, distance),\n      recommendations: this.generateRecommendations(entity, threat),\n      timestamp: Date.now(),\n    };\n  }\n\n  private getRelationType(\n    entityType: string,\n    threatType: string\n  ): string | null {\n    const relations: Record<string, Record<string, string>> = {\n      aircraft: {\n        conflict: 'aircraft_conflict',\n        air_defense: 'aircraft_conflict',\n        gps_jamming: 'aircraft_conflict',\n        no_fly_zone: 'aircraft_conflict',\n      },\n      vessel: {\n        chokepoint: 'vessel_chokepoint',\n        piracy: 'vessel_chokepoint',\n        sanctions: 'vessel_chokepoint',\n        maritime_dispute: 'vessel_chokepoint',\n      },\n      satellite: {\n        conflict: 'satellite_event',\n        natural_disaster: 'satellite_event',\n        military_exercise: 'satellite_event',\n      },\n      infrastructure: {\n        earthquake: 'infrastructure_quake',\n        tsunami: 'infrastructure_quake',\n        wildfire: 'infrastructure_quake',\n      },\n      any: {\n        cyber_attack: 'cyber_country',\n        malware: 'malware_region',\n        sanctions: 'cyber_country',\n      },\n    };\n\n    return relations[entityType]?.[threatType] || \n           relations.any?.[threatType] || null;\n  }\n\n  private calculateCorrelationScore(\n    entity: TrackedEntity,\n    threat: ThreatEvent,\n    distance: number,\n    timeDiffHours: number\n  ): number {\n    let score = 50; // Base\n\n    // Proximité spatiale (plus proche = plus haut score)\n    const spatialScore = Math.max(0, 100 - (distance / 200) * 100);\n    score += spatialScore * 0.4;\n\n    // Temporalité (plus récent = plus haut score)\n    const temporalScore = Math.max(0, 100 - timeDiffHours * 10);\n    score += temporalScore * 0.2;\n\n    // Sévérité de la menace\n    const severityScore: Record<string, number> = {\n      CRITICAL: 30,\n      HIGH: 20,\n      MEDIUM: 10,\n      LOW: 0,\n    };\n    score += severityScore[threat.severity] || 0;\n\n    // Bonus pour entités à risque\n    if (entity.type === 'vessel' && entity.label?.includes('tanker')) {\n      score += 10;\n    }\n    if (entity.type === 'aircraft' && entity.label?.includes('military')) {\n      score += 15;\n    }\n\n    return Math.min(100, score);\n  }\n\n  private getTimeWindow(relationType: string): number {\n    const windows: Record<string, number> = {\n      aircraft_conflict: 6,\n      vessel_chokepoint: 24,\n      satellite_event: 12,\n      infrastructure_quake: 48,\n      cyber_country: 72,\n      malware_region: 24,\n    };\n    return windows[relationType] || 24;\n  }\n\n  private determineSeverity(score: number): string {\n    if (score >= 85) return 'CRITICAL';\n    if (score >= 70) return 'HIGH';\n    if (score >= 50) return 'MEDIUM';\n    return 'LOW';\n  }\n\n  private generateDescription(entity: TrackedEntity, threat: ThreatEvent, distance: number): string {\n    return `${entity.label} (${entity.type}) is ${Math.round(distance)} km from ${threat.title} (${threat.type})`;\n  }\n\n  private generateRecommendations(entity: TrackedEntity, threat: ThreatEvent): string[] {\n    const recs: string[] = [];\n    \n    if (entity.type === 'aircraft') {\n      recs.push('Monitor flight trajectory changes');\n      recs.push('Check NOTAMs for airspace restrictions');\n      recs.push('Verify ADS-B signal integrity');\n    }\n    \n    if (entity.type === 'vessel') {\n      recs.push('Monitor AIS transmission status');\n      recs.push('Check port congestion and delays');\n      recs.push('Verify cargo manifest against sanctions');\n    }\n    \n    if (threat.severity === 'CRITICAL' || threat.severity === 'HIGH') {\n      recs.push('IMMEDIATE ACTION REQUIRED - Escalate to analyst');\n    }\n    \n    return recs;\n  }\n\n  private calculateDistance(a: Coord, b: Coord): number {\n    const R = 6371;\n    const dLat = (b.lat - a.lat) * Math.PI / 180;\n    const dLon = (b.lng - a.lng) * Math.PI / 180;\n    const lat1 = a.lat * Math.PI / 180;\n    const lat2 = b.lat * Math.PI / 180;\n    const h = Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 + Math.cos(lat1) * Math.cos(lat2) * Math.sin(dLon / 2) ** 2;\n    return 2 * R * Math.asin(Math.sqrt(h));\n  }\n}\n\ninterface TrackedEntity {\n  id: string;\n  type: string;\n  label: string;\n  lat: number;\n  lng: number;\n  timestamp: number;\n  metadata?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface ThreatEvent {\n  id: string;\n  type: string;\n  title: string;\n  severity: string;\n  lat: number;\n  lng: number;\n  timestamp: number;\n  country?: string;\n  iocs?: string[];\n}\n\ninterface GeoThreatCorrelation {\n  id: string;\n  entityId: string;\n  entityType: string;\n  entityLabel: string;\n  threatId: string;\n  threatType: string;\n  threatLabel: string;\n  relationType: string;\n  distance: number;\n  timeDiffHours: number;\n  score: number;\n  severity: string;\n  description: string;\n  recommendations: string[];\n  timestamp: number;\n}\n```\n\n### 3.3 Threat Scoring\n\n```python\n# ── Threat Scorer (Python/FastAPI) ──\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nclass ThreatScorer:\n    \"\"\"Scoreur de menace composite basé sur facteurs multiples.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        self.severity_weights = {\n            'CRITICAL': 1.0,\n            'HIGH': 0.75,\n            'MEDIUM': 0.5,\n            'LOW': 0.25\n        }\n        \n    def calculate_threat_score(\n        self,\n        threat_type: str,\n        severity: str,\n        distance_km: Optional[float],\n        time_delta_hours: float,\n        entity_type: str,\n        entity_risk_score: float,\n        context_factors: dict\n    ) -> dict:\n        \"\"\"\n        Calcule un score de menace composite.\n        \n        Args:\n            threat_type: Type de menace (cyber, physical, etc.)\n            severity: Sévérité de la menace\n            distance_km: Distance entre l'entité et la menace (optionnel)\n            time_delta_hours: Écart temporel\n            entity_type: Type d'entité\n            entity_risk_score: Score de risque de l'entité (0-100)\n            context_factors: Facteurs contextuels additionnels\n            \n        Returns:\n            Dict contenant le score et les métriques détaillées\n        \"\"\"\n        # 1. Score de base basé sur la sévérité\n        base_score = self.severity_weights.get(severity.upper(), 0.25) * 100\n        \n        # 2. Bonus de proximité spatiale\n        spatial_score = 0\n        if distance_km is not None:\n            if distance_km < 10:\n                spatial_score = 30\n            elif distance_km < 50:\n                spatial_score = 20\n            elif distance_km < 100:\n                spatial_score = 15\n            elif distance_km < 250:\n                spatial_score = 10\n            elif distance_km < 500:\n                spatial_score = 5\n        \n        # 3. Bonus temporel\n        temporal_score = max(0, 50 - time_delta_hours * 2)\n        \n        # 4. Score de risque entité\n        entity_bonus = entity_risk_score * 0.3\n        \n        # 5. Facteurs contextuels\n        context_bonus = 0\n        if context_factors.get('in_conflict_zone'):\n            context_bonus += 20\n        if context_factors.get('near_critical_infrastructure'):\n            context_bonus += 15\n        if context_factors.get('previous_alerts', 0) > 3:\n            context_bonus += 10\n        if context_factors.get('sanctioned_country'):\n            context_bonus += 25\n        if context_factors.get('gps_jamming_detected'):\n            context_bonus += 20\n        if context_factors.get('ais_silence'):\n            context_bonus += 15\n            \n        # Score composite\n        final_score = min(100, (\n            base_score * 0.35 +\n            spatial_score * 0.20 +\n            temporal_score * 0.10 +\n            entity_bonus * 0.15 +\n            context_bonus * 0.20\n        ))\n        \n        return {\n            'final_score': round(final_score),\n            'components': {\n                'base_score': round(base_score),\n                'spatial_score': spatial_score,\n                'temporal_score': round(temporal_score),\n                'entity_bonus': round(entity_bonus),\n                'context_bonus': context_bonus\n            },\n            'severity': self._determine_severity(final_score),\n            'confidence': self._calculate_confidence(\n                distance_km is not None,\n                time_delta_hours,\n                context_factors\n            )\n        }\n    \n    def _determine_severity(self, score: float) -> str:\n        if score >= 85:\n            return 'CRITICAL'\n        elif score >= 70:\n            return 'HIGH'\n        elif score >= 50:\n            return 'MEDIUM'\n        elif score >= 30:\n            return 'LOW'\n        return 'INFO'\n    \n    def _calculate_confidence(\n        self,\n        has_geo: bool,\n        time_delta: float,\n        context: dict\n    ) -> int:\n        confidence = 50  # Base\n        \n        if has_geo:\n            confidence += 20\n        if time_delta < 1:\n            confidence += 15\n        elif time_delta < 6:\n            confidence += 10\n        elif time_delta < 24:\n            confidence += 5\n            \n        if context.get('verified_source'):\n            confidence += 15\n        if context.get('multiple_sources'):\n            confidence += 10\n            \n        return min(100, confidence)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Pipeline de Données Géospatiales\n\n### 4.1 Data Pipeline Complet\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                     GEOSPATIAL DATA PIPELINE                                 │\n│                                                                              │\n│  1. INGESTION                                                                │\n│  ┌────────────┐ ┌────────────┐ ┌────────────┐ ┌────────────┐               │\n│  │ ADS-B      │ │ AIS Stream │ │ Satellites  │ │ CCTV Feeds │               │\n│  │ (OpenSky)  │ │ (AIS Hub)  │ │ (Celestrak) │ │ (RTSP)     │               │\n│  └──────┬─────┘ └──────┬─────┘ └──────┬─────┘ └──────┬─────┘               │\n│         │              │              │              │                      │\n│         ▼              ▼              ▼              ▼                      │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │           STREAM NORMALIZER → GeoJSON FeatureCollection              │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                    │                                         │\n│  2. INDEXATION SPATIALE                                                     │\n│                                    ▼                                         │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │           H3 SPATIAL INDEX + QuadTree                               │   │\n│  │  Indexe chaque entité dans une cellule H3 résolution 8 (~0.7km²)    │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                    │                                         │\n│  3. TRAJECTOIRE & ANALYSE                                                   │\n│                                    ▼                                         │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │           TRAJECTORY ANALYZER                                        │   │\n│  │  - Maintient l'historique des positions (rolling window 60 min)      │   │\n│  │  - Calcule vitesse, cap, accélération                                │   │\n│  │  - Détecte loitering, virages serrés, anomalies de vitesse           │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                    │                                         │\n│  4. GEOFENCE CHECKUP                                                        │\n│                                    ▼                                         │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │           GEOFENCE ENGINE                                             │   │\n│  │  - Vérifie chaque position contre les zones définies                  │   │\n│  │  - Zones: conflit, chokepoint, espace aérien restreint, sanctions    │   │\n│  │  - Génère GeoFenceAlert si entrée dans zone sensible                 │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                    │                                         │\n│  5. CORRÉLATION GÉO-MENACE                                                  │\n│                                    ▼                                         │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │           GEO-THREAT CORRELATOR                                       │   │\n│  │  - Corrèle positions avec menaces actives                             │   │\n│  │  - Utilise seuils spatiaux (50-500km) et temporels (6-72h)           │   │\n│  │  - Génère GeoThreatCorrelation avec score composite                  │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                    │                                         │\n│  6. SCORING & ALERTE                                                        │\n│                                    ▼                                         │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │           THREAT SCORER                                               │   │\n│  │  - Score composite: base ×0.35 + spatial ×0.20 + temporel ×0.10     │   │\n│  │  - Bonus contextuels: conflit, infrastructure, sanctions              │   │\n│  │  - Alerte si score ≥ 70 (HIGH) ou ≥ 85 (CRITICAL)                    │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                    │                                         │\n│  7. PUBLICATION                                                             │\n│                                    ▼                                         │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │           DELIVERY                                                    │   │\n│  │  ┌────────────┐ ┌────────────┐ ┌────────────┐ ┌────────────┐        │   │\n│  │  │ Redis      │ │ WebSocket  │ │ Neo4j      │ │ Tile       │        │   │\n│  │  │ osiris:intel│ │ Frontend   │ │ Graph DB   │ │ Server     │        │   │\n│  │  │ :geo-threat│ │ Dashboard  │ │ Relations  │ │ Vector     │        │   │\n│  │  └────────────┘ └────────────┘ └────────────┘ └────────────┘        │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 4.2 FastAPI GEOINT Service\n\n```python\n# ── GEOINT Service Principal (FastAPI) ──\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, WebSocket, HTTPException, Query\nfrom fastapi.responses import JSONResponse\nimport redis.asyncio as redis\nimport json\nfrom typing import Optional\nimport asyncio\nfrom datetime import datetime\n\napp = FastAPI(title=\"OSIRIS GEOINT Engine\", version=\"4.2\")\n\n# Redis client\nredis_client = redis.from_url(\"redis://[REDACTED:PASSWORD]@app.get(\"/health\")\nasync def health():\n    \"\"\"Health check du service.\"\"\"\n    return {\n        \"status\": \"ok\",\n        \"service\": \"geoint-engine\",\n        \"version\": \"4.2\",\n        \"timestamp\": datetime.utcnow().isoformat()\n    }\n\n@app.post(\"/api/geo/ingest\")\nasync def ingest_position(data: TrackedEntity):\n    \"\"\"\n    Ingère une position d'entité géographique.\n    \n    Trajectory → Geofence Check → Correlation → Alert\n    \"\"\"\n    # 1. Indexer spatialement\n    h3_cell = spatial_index.index_entity(data)\n    \n    # 2. Enregistrer trajectoire\n    trajectory_analyzer.record_position(data.id, {\n        \"lat\": data.lat,\n        \"lng\": data.lng,\n        \"speed\": data.speed,\n        \"heading\": data.heading,\n        \"timestamp\": data.timestamp,\n    })\n    \n    # 3. Analyser trajectoire\n    anomalies = trajectory_analyzer.analyze_trajectory(data.id)\n    \n    # 4. Vérifier geofences\n    fence_alerts = geofence_engine.check_position(data)\n    \n    # 5. Publier sur Redis\n    await redis_client.xadd(\n        \"osiris:geo:positions\",\n        {\n            \"entity_id\": data.id,\n            \"entity_type\": data.type,\n            \"data\": data.model_dump_json(),\n            \"h3_cell\": h3_cell,\n            \"anomalies\": json.dumps(anomalies),\n            \"fence_alerts\": json.dumps(fence_alerts),\n            \"timestamp\": datetime.utcnow().isoformat()\n        }\n    )\n    \n    return {\n        \"status\": \"ingested\",\n        \"h3_cell\": h3_cell,\n        \"anomalies\": anomalies,\n        \"fence_alerts\": fence_alerts\n    }\n\n@app.post(\"/api/geo/correlate\")\nasync def correlate_geo_threat(\n    radius_km: float = Query(100, description=\"Rayon de recherche\"),\n    time_window_hours: float = Query(6, description=\"Fenêtre temporelle\")\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Corrèle les positions géographiques avec les menaces actives.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Récupérer les entités récentes\n    entities = await get_recent_entities(time_window_hours)\n    \n    # Récupérer les menaces actives\n    threats = await get_active_threats(time_window_hours)\n    \n    # Corréler\n    correlations = await geo_threat_correlator.correlate(entities, threats)\n    \n    # Filtrer par rayon\n    filtered = [c for c in correlations if c.distance <= radius_km]\n    \n    # Publier les corrélations fortes\n    strong_correlations = [c for c in filtered if c.score >= 70]\n    for corr in strong_correlations:\n        await redis_client.xadd(\n            \"osiris:intel:geo-threat\",\n            {\"data\": corr.model_dump_json()}\n        )\n    \n    return {\n        \"total_correlations\": len(filtered),\n        \"strong_correlations\": len(strong_correlations),\n        \"correlations\": filtered[:50]  # Top 50\n    }\n\n@app.get(\"/api/geo/clusters\")\nasync def get_clusters(\n    resolution: int = Query(8, ge=6, le=10),\n    min_density: int = Query(5, ge=1),\n    bbox: Optional[str] = Query(None, description=\"west,south,east,north\")\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Retourne les clusters géospatiaux.\n    \"\"\"\n    entities = await get_all_entities()\n    \n    if bbox:\n        coords = [float(x) for x in bbox.split(\",\")]\n        entities = [\n            e for e in entities \n            if coords[0] <= e.lng <= coords[2] \n            and coords[1] <= e.lat <= coords[3]\n        ]\n    \n    clusters = spatial_index.cluster_by_density(entities, min_density)\n    \n    return {\"clusters\": clusters}\n\n@app.get(\"/api/geo/trajectory/{entity_id}\")\nasync def get_trajectory(entity_id: str, minutes: int = Query(30)):\n    \"\"\"\n    Retourne l'historique de trajectoire d'une entité.\n    \"\"\"\n    trajectory = trajectory_analyzer.get_trajectory(entity_id, minutes)\n    return {\"entity_id\": entity_id, \"trajectory\": trajectory}\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n# WEBSOCKET ENDPOINT\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n@app.websocket(\"/ws/geo\")\nasync def geo_websocket(websocket: WebSocket):\n    \"\"\"Stream temps réel des positions et alertes géo.\"\"\"\n    await websocket.accept()\n    \n    async def redis_listener():\n        pubsub = redis_client.pubsub()\n        await pubsub.subscribe(\"osiris:geo:positions\", \"osiris:intel:geo-threat\", \"osiris:alerts\")\n        \n        async for message in pubsub.listen():\n            if message[\"type\"] == \"message\":\n                try:\n                    await websocket.send_json({\n                        \"channel\": message[\"channel\"],\n                        \"data\": json.loads(message[\"data\"]),\n                        \"timestamp\": datetime.utcnow().isoformat()\n                    })\n                except Exception:\n                    break\n    \n    try:\n        await redis_listener()\n    except Exception:\n        await websocket.close()\n\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n# FONCTIONS AIDE\n# ─────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nasync def get_recent_entities(hours: float) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Récupère les entités actives récentes depuis Redis.\"\"\"\n    entities = []\n    cursor = 0\n    while True:\n        cursor, keys = await redis_client.scan(cursor, match=\"entity:*\", count=100)\n        for key in keys:\n            data = await redis_client.get(key)\n            if data:\n                entity = json.loads(data)\n                age_hours = (datetime.utcnow() - datetime.fromisoformat(\n                    entity.get(\"last_seen\", datetime.utcnow().isoformat())\n                )).total_seconds() / 3600\n                if age_hours <= hours:\n                    entities.append(entity)\n        if cursor == 0:\n            break\n    return entities\n\nasync def get_active_threats(hours: float) -> list:\n    \"\"\"Récupère les menaces actives depuis les streams Redis.\"\"\"\n    threats = []\n    # Lire les menaces récentes du stream\n    messages = await redis_client.xrevrange(\n        \"osiris:threat:active\", \n        count=100\n    )\n    for msg_id, msg_data in messages:\n        threat = json.loads(msg_data.get(\"data\", \"{}\"))\n        age_hours = (datetime.utcnow() - datetime.fromisoformat(\n            threat.get(\"timestamp\", datetime.utcnow().isoformat())\n        )).total_seconds() / 3600\n        if age_hours <= hours:\n            threats.append(threat)\n    return threats\n\nasync def get_all_entities() -> list:\n    \"\"\"Récupère toutes les entités géographiques.\"\"\"\n    entities = []\n    cursor = 0\n    while True:\n        cursor, keys = await redis_client.scan(cursor, match=\"entity:*\", count=500)\n        for key in keys:\n            data = await redis_client.get(key)\n            if data:\n                entities.append(json.loads(data))\n        if cursor == 0:\n            break\n    return entities\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Détection d'Anomalies\n\n### 5.1 Types d'Anomalies\n\n```\nAnomalies de Mouvement\n├── LOITERING          → Entité stationnaire ou quasi-stationnaire >10 min\n│   ├── Seuil: vitesse < 5 km/h, durée > 10 min\n│   ├── Contexte normal: navire au port, avion au parking\n│   └── Contexte anormal: navire en mer sans AIS, avion en vol\n│\n├── SHARP_TURN         → Changement de cap > 120°\n│   ├── Seuil: 120° (MEDIUM), 160° (HIGH)\n│   ├── Contexte normal: approche aéroport, manœuvre portuaire\n│   └── Contexte anormal: en pleine mer, à haute altitude\n│\n├── SPEED_ANOMALY      → Vitesse anormale (Z-score > 2.5)\n│   ├── Seuil: Z-score 2.5 (MEDIUM), 4.0 (HIGH)\n│   ├── Contexte normal: accélération/décélération graduelle\n│   └── Contexte anormal: changement brusque inexpliqué\n│\n├── RAPID_DESCENT      → Descente rapide (>30 m/s) pour aéronefs\n│   ├── Seuil: 30 m/s (HIGH), 50 m/s (CRITICAL)\n│   └── Peut indiquer: urgence, perte de pression, problème technique\n│\n└── GEO_FENCE_ENTRY    → Entrée dans zone sensible\n    ├── Zones: conflit, chokepoint, espace aérien restreint\n    ├── Alerte immédiate avec cooldown 5 min\n    └── Escalade selon sévérité de la zone\n\nAnomalies de Localisation\n├── PORT_UNKNOWN       → Navire faisant escale dans un port non-enregistré\n│   └── Détection: AIS destination ≠ ports connus\n│\n├── AIS_SILENCE        → Navire cessant d'émettre AIS en zone sensible\n│   └── Détection: perte de signal AIS dans chokepoint ou conflit\n│\n├── GPS_SPOOFING       → Coordonnées incohérentes (avion au-dessus de sa vitesse)\n│   └── Détection: impossibilité physique (ex: sol → 10km en 30s)\n│\n└── SQUAWK_EMERGENCY   → Code transpondeur 7500/7600/7700\n    └── Détection: ADS-B category = emergency\n```\n\n### 5.2 Implémentation du Détecteur\n\n```python\n# ── Anomaly Detection Engine ──\nimport numpy as np\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\nfrom typing import List, Optional, Tuple\n\nclass AnomalyDetector:\n    \"\"\"Détecteur d'anomalies multi-modèle.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        self.trajectory_analyzer = TrajectoryAnalyzer()\n        self.models = {\n            'statistical': StatisticalAnomalyModel(),\n            'ml': MLAnomalyModel(),\n            'rule': RuleBasedModel(),\n        }\n    \n    async def analyze_entity(\n        self,\n        entity: TrackedEntity,\n        history: List[PositionRecord]\n    ) -> List[Anomaly]:\n        \"\"\"Analyse complète d'une entité pour toutes anomalies.\"\"\"\n        anomalies = []\n        \n        # 1. Anomalies statistiques (Z-score, IQR)\n        stat_anomalies = self.models['statistical'].analyze(entity, history)\n        anomalies.extend(stat_anomalies)\n        \n        # 2. Anomalies basées sur règles\n        rule_anomalies = self.models['rule'].analyze(entity, history)\n        anomalies.extend(rule_anomalies)\n        \n        # 3. Anomalies ML (si modèle disponible)\n        ml_anomalies = self.models['ml'].analyze(entity, history)\n        anomalies.extend(ml_anomalies)\n        \n        # 4. Corrélation inter-anomalies\n        return self.correlate_anomalies(anomalies)\n    \n    def correlate_anomalies(self, anomalies: List[Anomaly]) -> List[Anomaly]:\n        \"\"\"Corrèle les anomalies entre elles pour renforcer les signaux.\"\"\"\n        if len(anomalies) < 2:\n            return anomalies\n        \n        # Regrouper par fenêtre temporelle (5 min)\n        grouped = {}\n        for anomaly in anomalies:\n            time_key = int(anomaly.timestamp / 300) * 300\n            if time_key not in grouped:\n                grouped[time_key] = []\n            grouped[time_key].append(anomaly)\n        \n        correlated = []\n        for time_key, group in grouped.items():\n            if len(group) >= 2:\n                # Plusieurs anomalies simultanées = score renforcé\n                avg_score = sum(a.score for a in group) / len(group)\n                boost = min(20, len(group) * 10)  # +10 par anomalie, max +20\n                for anomaly in group:\n                    anomaly.score = min(100, anomaly.score + boost)\n                    anomaly.confidence = min(100, anomaly.confidence + 10)\n                    anomaly.correlated = True\n            correlated.extend(group)\n        \n        return correlated\n\n\nclass StatisticalAnomalyModel:\n    \"\"\"Modèle statistique (Z-score, IQR, moving average).\"\"\"\n    \n    def analyze(self, entity: TrackedEntity, history: List[PositionRecord]) -> List[Anomaly]:\n        anomalies = []\n        \n        if len(history) < 10:\n            return anomalies\n        \n        # Analyser les vitesses\n        speeds = [\n            self._calculate_speed(history[i], history[i+1])\n            for i in range(len(history) - 1)\n            if history[i].timestamp > 0\n        ]\n        \n        if speeds:\n            # Z-score sur la dernière vitesse\n            mean = np.mean(speeds[:-1])\n            std = np.std(speeds[:-1])\n            last_speed = speeds[-1]\n            \n            if std > 0:\n                z_score = abs(last_speed - mean) / std\n                if z_score > 2.5:\n                    anomalies.append(Anomaly(\n                        entity_id=entity.id,\n                        type='SPEED_ANOMALY',\n                        severity='HIGH' if z_score > 4 else 'MEDIUM',\n                        score=min(100, 50 + z_score * 10),\n                        confidence=min(85, 50 + z_score * 8),\n                        description=f\"Speed Z-score: {z_score:.2f}\",\n                        position={'lat': entity.lat, 'lng': entity.lng},\n                        timestamp=entity.timestamp,\n                        model='statistical'\n                    ))\n        \n        return anomalies\n    \n    def _calculate_speed(self, a: PositionRecord, b: PositionRecord) -> float:\n        \"\"\"Calcule la vitesse entre deux positions (km/h).\"\"\"\n        if a.timestamp == b.timestamp:\n            return 0\n        dist = self._haversine(a.lat, a.lng, b.lat, b.lng)\n        time_hours = abs(b.timestamp - a.timestamp) / 3600\n        return dist / time_hours if time_hours > 0 else 0\n    \n    def _haversine(self, lat1: float, lon1: float, lat2: float, lon2: float) -> float:\n        R = 6371\n        dlat = np.radians(lat2 - lat1)\n        dlon = np.radians(lon2 - lon1)\n        a = np.sin(dlat/2)**2 + np.cos(np.radians(lat1)) * np.cos(np.radians(lat2)) * np.sin(dlon/2)**2\n        return 2 * R * np.arcsin(np.sqrt(a))\n\n\nclass RuleBasedModel:\n    \"\"\"Modèle basé sur des règles expertes.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        self.rules = [\n            LoiteringRule(min_speed=5, min_duration_minutes=10),\n            SharpTurnRule(min_angle=120),\n            GeofenceEntryRule(),\n        ]\n    \n    def analyze(self, entity: TrackedEntity, history: List[PositionRecord]) -> List[Anomaly]:\n        anomalies = []\n        for rule in self.rules:\n            anomaly = rule.evaluate(entity, history)\n            if anomaly:\n                anomaly.model = 'rule'\n                anomalies.append(anomaly)\n        return anomalies\n\n\nclass LoiteringRule:\n    \"\"\"Règle: détection de loitering.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, min_speed: float = 5, min_duration_minutes: float = 10):\n        self.min_speed = min_speed\n        self.min_duration = min_duration_minutes * 60\n    \n    def evaluate(self, entity: TrackedEntity, history: List[PositionRecord]) -> Optional[Anomaly]:\n        if len(history) < 5:\n            return None\n        \n        recent = history[-10:]  # 10 derniers points\n        total_distance = 0\n        for i in range(1, len(recent)):\n            total_distance += self._haversine(\n                recent[i-1].lat, recent[i-1].lng,\n                recent[i].lat, recent[i].lng\n            )\n        \n        avg_speed = total_distance / len(recent)\n        duration = (recent[-1].timestamp - recent[0].timestamp)\n        \n        if avg_speed < self.min_speed and duration > self.min_duration:\n            severity = 'HIGH' if avg_speed < 1 else 'MEDIUM'\n            return Anomaly(\n                entity_id=entity.id,\n                type='LOITERING',\n                severity=severity,\n                score=min(100, 40 + (self.min_duration / duration) * 30),\n                confidence=min(90, 50 + (duration / self.min_duration) * 20),\n                description=f\"Loitering: {avg_speed:.1f} km/h over {duration/60:.0f} min\",\n                position={'lat': entity.lat, 'lng': entity.lng},\n                timestamp=entity.timestamp,\n                model='rule'\n            )\n        return None\n    \n    def _haversine(self, lat1, lon1, lat2, lon2):\n        R = 6371\n        dlat = np.radians(lat2 - lat1)\n        dlon = np.radians(lon2 - lon1)\n        a = np.sin(dlat/2)**2 + np.cos(np.radians(lat1)) * np.cos(np.radians(lat2)) * np.sin(dlon/2)**2\n        return 2 * R * np.arcsin(np.sqrt(a))\n\n\nclass MLAnomalyModel:\n    \"\"\"Modèle ML (DBSCAN, Isolation Forest, LSTM).\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        self.model = None  # Placeholder pour modèle ML\n        self.is_trained = False\n    \n    def analyze(self, entity: TrackedEntity, history: List[PositionRecord]) -> List[Anomaly]:\n        # Placeholder: à implémenter avec un modèle ML entraîné\n        return []\n\n\nclass Anomaly:\n    \"\"\"Structure d'une anomalie détectée.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        entity_id: str,\n        type: str,\n        severity: str,\n        score: float,\n        confidence: float,\n        description: str,\n        position: dict,\n        timestamp: float,\n        model: str,\n        correlated: bool = False\n    ):\n        self.entity_id = entity_id\n        self.type = type\n        self.severity = severity\n        self.score = score\n        self.confidence = confidence\n        self.description = description\n        self.position = position\n        self.timestamp = timestamp\n        self.model = model\n        self.correlated = correlated\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. Système d'Alertes\n\n### 6.1 Architecture des Alertes\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                         ALERT SYSTEM                                        │\n│                                                                              │\n│  Sources d'Alertes                                                           │\n│  ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐ ┌──────────────┐       │\n│  │ GEOINT       │ │ Threat       │ │ Anomaly      │ │ GeoFence     │       │\n│  │ Engine       │ │ Engine       │ │ Detector     │ │ Engine       │       │\n│  └──────┬───────┘ └──────┬───────┘ └──────┬───────┘ └──────┬───────┘       │\n│         │               │               │               │                  │\n│         └───────┬───────┴───────┬───────┴───────┬───────┘                  │\n│                 │               │               │                           │\n│                 ▼               ▼               ▼                           │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │                      ALERT FUSION ENGINE                              │   │\n│  │                                                                       │   │\n│  │  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐                │   │\n│  │  │ Dedup        │  │ Priority     │  │ Enrichment   │                │   │\n│  │  │ (same entity │──▶│ Calculator   │──▶│ (Context,    │                │   │\n│  │  │  + type)     │   │ (Urgency)    │   │  History)    │                │   │\n│  │  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘                │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n│                                    │                                         │\n│                                    ▼                                         │\n│  ┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐   │\n│  │                      ALERT DISPATCHER                                │   │\n│  │                                                                       │   │\n│  │  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐  ┌──────────────┐                │   │\n│  │  │ CRITICAL     │  │ HIGH         │  │ MEDIUM/LOW   │                │   │\n│  │  │ WebSocket +  │──▶│ WebSocket +  │──▶│ WebSocket +  │                │   │\n│  │  │ Notification │   │ Dashboard    │   │ Log only    │                │   │\n│  │  │ + SMS/Email  │   │ Flash        │   │             │                │   │\n│  │  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘  └──────────────┘                │   │\n│  └──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘   │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 6.2 Alert Fusion Engine\n\n```python\n# ── Alert Fusion Engine ──\nfrom datetime import datetime, timedelta\nfrom typing import List, Optional\n\nclass AlertFusionEngine:\n    \"\"\"Moteur de fusion et priorisation des alertes.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self):\n        self.alert_history: dict = {}  # entity_id → last alerts\n        self.dedup_window = 300  # 5 min\n        self.cooldowns: dict = {}  # alert_type:entity_id → timestamp\n        \n    async def process_alert(self, alert: GeospatialAlert) -> Optional[ProcessedAlert]:\n        \"\"\"Traite et fusionne une alerte entrante.\"\"\"\n        \n        # 1. Déduplication\n        if self._is_duplicate(alert):\n            return None\n        \n        # 2. Vérifier cooldown\n        if self._is_cooldown(alert):\n            return None\n        \n        # 3. Enrichir avec contexte\n        enriched = await self._enrich_alert(alert)\n        \n        # 4. Calculer priorité\n        priority = self._calculate_priority(enriched)\n        \n        # 5. Mettre à jour l'historique\n        self._update_history(alert)\n        \n        # 6. Appliquer cooldown\n        self._apply_cooldown(alert)\n        \n        return ProcessedAlert(\n            id=f\"alert:{alert.entity_id}:{int(datetime.utcnow().timestamp())}\",\n            source=alert.source,\n            type=alert.type,\n            severity=enriched.severity,\n            priority=priority,\n            entity_id=alert.entity_id,\n            entity_type=alert.entity_type,\n            description=alert.description,\n            score=alert.score,\n            confidence=alert.confidence,\n            position=alert.position,\n            enriched_context=enriched.context,\n            recommendations=enriched.recommendations,\n            timestamp=datetime.utcnow().isoformat(),\n            expires_at=(datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(hours=enriched.ttl_hours)).isoformat()\n        )\n    \n    def _is_duplicate(self, alert: GeospatialAlert) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Vérifie si l'alerte est un doublon.\"\"\"\n        key = f\"{alert.entity_id}:{alert.type}\"\n        last_alert = self.alert_history.get(key)\n        if last_alert:\n            time_diff = (datetime.utcnow() - last_alert).total_seconds()\n            if time_diff < self.dedup_window:\n                return True\n        return False\n    \n    def _is_cooldown(self, alert: GeospatialAlert) -> bool:\n        \"\"\"Vérifie si l'alerte est en cooldown.\"\"\"\n        key = f\"{alert.type}:{alert.entity_id}\"\n        cooldown_end = self.cooldowns.get(key)\n        if cooldown_end and datetime.utcnow() < cooldown_end:\n            return True\n        return False\n    \n    def _apply_cooldown(self, alert: GeospatialAlert):\n        \"\"\"Applique un cooldown basé sur la sévérité.\"\"\"\n        key = f\"{alert.type}:{alert.entity_id}\"\n        severity_cooldowns = {\n            'CRITICAL': 600,   # 10 min\n            'HIGH': 300,       # 5 min\n            'MEDIUM': 120,     # 2 min\n            'LOW': 60,         # 1 min\n        }\n        cooldown = severity_cooldowns.get(alert.severity, 60)\n        self.cooldowns[key] = datetime.utcnow() + timedelta(seconds=cooldown)\n    \n    async def _enrich_alert(self, alert: GeospatialAlert) -> EnrichedAlert:\n        \"\"\"Enrichit une alerte avec contexte.\"\"\"\n        context = {}\n        recommendations = []\n        \n        # Contexte géographique\n        if alert.position:\n            context['nearby_conflicts'] = await self._get_nearby_conflicts(\n                alert.position['lat'], alert.position['lng'], 200\n            )\n            context['country'] = await self._reverse_geocode(\n                alert.position['lat'], alert.position['lng']\n            )\n        \n        # Contexte entité\n        context['entity_history'] = await self._get_entity_alert_history(\n            alert.entity_id\n        )\n        \n        # Recommandations adaptatives\n        if alert.severity == 'CRITICAL':\n            recommendations.append(\"ESCALADE IMMÉDIATE - Notifier analyste senior\")\n            recommendations.append(\"Activer monitoring temps réel\")\n        elif alert.severity == 'HIGH':\n            recommendations.append(\"Priorité haute - Analyser dans l'heure\")\n            recommendations.append(\"Vérifier corrélations avec autres alertes\")\n        \n        if alert.type == 'GEO_FENCE_ENTRY':\n            recommendations.append(\"Vérifier autorisation de passage\")\n            recommendations.append(\"Contacter contrôle si nécessaire\")\n        \n        # TTL basé sur sévérité\n        ttl_hours = {'CRITICAL': 24, 'HIGH': 12, 'MEDIUM': 6, 'LOW': 3}\n        \n        return EnrichedAlert(\n            severity=alert.severity,\n            context=context,\n            recommendations=recommendations,\n            ttl_hours=ttl_hours.get(alert.severity, 6)\n        )\n    \n    def _calculate_priority(self, enriched: EnrichedAlert) -> int:\n        \"\"\"Calcule la priorité (1-100).\"\"\"\n        priority = 50  # Base\n        \n        severity_priority = {\n            'CRITICAL': 40, 'HIGH': 30, 'MEDIUM': 15, 'LOW': 5\n        }\n        priority += severity_priority.get(enriched.severity, 5)\n        \n        # Bonus pour contexte à risque\n        if enriched.context.get('nearby_conflicts'):\n            priority += 20\n        if enriched.context.get('entity_history', 0) > 3:\n            priority += 10\n        \n        return min(100, priority)\n    \n    def _update_history(self, alert: GeospatialAlert):\n        \"\"\"Met à jour l'historique des alertes.\"\"\"\n        key = f\"{alert.entity_id}:{alert.type}\"\n        self.alert_history[key] = datetime.utcnow()\n        \n        # Nettoyage (garder 1 heure)\n        cutoff = datetime.utcnow() - timedelta(hours=1)\n        self.alert_history = {\n            k: v for k, v in self.alert_history.items() \n            if v > cutoff\n        }\n\n\nclass GeospatialAlert:\n    \"\"\"Alerte géospatiale brute.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        source: str,\n        type: str,\n        severity: str,\n        entity_id: str,\n        entity_type: str,\n        description: str,\n        score: float,\n        confidence: float,\n        position: Optional[dict] = None\n    ):\n        self.source = source\n        self.type = type\n        self.severity = severity\n        self.entity_id = entity_id\n        self.entity_type = entity_type\n        self.description = description\n        self.score = score\n        self.confidence = confidence\n        self.position = position\n\n\nclass EnrichedAlert:\n    \"\"\"Alerte enrichie avec contexte.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(\n        self,\n        severity: str,\n        context: dict,\n        recommendations: List[str],\n        ttl_hours: int\n    ):\n        self.severity = severity\n        self.context = context\n        self.recommendations = recommendations\n        self.ttl_hours = ttl_hours\n\n\nclass ProcessedAlert:\n    \"\"\"Alerte finale traitée et prête à être dispatchée.\"\"\"\n    def __init__(self, **kwargs):\n        self.__dict__.update(kwargs)\n```\n\n### 6.3 Alert Dispatcher\n\n```python\n# ── Alert Dispatcher ──\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport logging\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\nclass AlertDispatcher:\n    \"\"\"Distribue les alertes vers les canaux appropriés.\"\"\"\n    \n    def __init__(self, redis_url: str = \"redis://redis:6379\"):\n        self.redis = None\n        self.redis_url = redis_url\n        self.channels = {\n            'CRITICAL': ['websocket', 'notification', 'webhook', 'log'],\n            'HIGH': ['websocket', 'dashboard', 'log'],\n            'MEDIUM': ['websocket', 'log'],\n            'LOW': ['log'],\n        }\n    \n    async def dispatch(self, alert: ProcessedAlert):\n        \"\"\"Dispatch une alerte vers tous les canaux configurés.\"\"\"\n        \n        if not self.redis:\n            import redis.asyncio as redis\n            self.redis = redis.from_url(self.redis_url)\n        \n        channels = self.channels.get(alert.severity, ['log'])\n        tasks = []\n        \n        if 'websocket' in channels:\n            tasks.append(self._dispatch_websocket(alert))\n        if 'notification' in channels:\n            tasks.append(self._dispatch_notification(alert))\n        if 'webhook' in channels:\n            tasks.append(self._dispatch_webhook(alert))\n        if 'dashboard' in channels:\n            tasks.append(self._dispatch_dashboard(alert))\n        if 'log' in channels:\n            tasks.append(self._dispatch_log(alert))\n        \n        await asyncio.gather(*tasks)\n    \n    async def _dispatch_websocket(self, alert: ProcessedAlert):\n        \"\"\"Publie sur le canal WebSocket.\"\"\"\n        await self.redis.publish(\n            'osiris:alerts',\n            json.dumps({\n                'type': 'geo_threat_alert',\n                'data': alert.__dict__,\n                'timestamp': datetime.utcnow().isoformat()\n            })\n        )\n        logger.info(f\"[WS] Alert dispatched: {alert.id}\")\n    \n    async def _dispatch_notification(self, alert: ProcessedAlert):\n        \"\"\"Envoie une notification push (SMS/Email).\"\"\"\n        # À implémenter: intégration Twilio/SendGrid\n        logger.info(f\"[NOTIF] Alert {alert.id} would send SMS/email\")\n    \n    async def _dispatch_webhook(self, alert: ProcessedAlert):\n        \"\"\"Appelle les webhooks externes configurés.\"\"\"\n        # Récupérer les webhooks depuis Redis\n        webhooks = await self.redis.smembers('config:webhooks:critical')\n        \n        for webhook in webhooks:\n            try:\n                import aiohttp\n                async with aiohttp.ClientSession() as session:\n                    await session.post(\n                        webhook,\n                        json={\n                            'event': 'geo_threat_alert',\n                            'alert': alert.__dict__\n                        },\n                        timeout=aiohttp.ClientTimeout(total=5)\n                    )\n                logger.info(f\"[WEBHOOK] Called {webhook}\")\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.error(f\"[WEBHOOK] Failed {webhook}: {e}\")\n    \n    async def _dispatch_dashboard(self, alert: ProcessedAlert):\n        \"\"\"Met à jour le tableau de bord.\"\"\"\n        await self.redis.xadd(\n            'osiris:dashboard:alerts',\n            {'data': json.dumps(alert.__dict__)}\n        )\n    \n    async def _dispatch_log(self, alert: ProcessedAlert):\n        \"\"\"Log l'alerte.\"\"\"\n        logger.warning(\n            f\"[ALERT] {alert.severity} | {alert.type} | \"\n            f\"{alert.entity_type}:{alert.entity_id} | \"\n            f\"{alert.description} | Score: {alert.score}\"\n        )\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Intégration au Graphe Global\n\n### 7.1 Schéma Neo4j pour GEOINT + Threat\n\n```cypher\n// ── Nœuds GEOINT ──\n// :GeoEntity { id, type, lat, lon, last_seen, speed, heading, status }\n// :Track { id, entity_id, points, start_time, end_time, avg_speed, max_speed }\n// :Cluster { id, center_lat, center_lon, radius, density, h3_cell }\n// :GeoFence { id, name, type, polygon, severity }\n\n// ── Nœuds Threat ──\n// :Threat { id, type, title, severity, lat, lon, timestamp }\n// :CVE { id, cvss, severity, published, in_kev }\n// :IOC { type, value, threat_type, first_seen, source }\n// :Sanction { entity, regime, type, date, authority }\n\n// ── Relations ──\n// (e:GeoEntity)-[:LOCATED_IN]->(c:Cluster)\n// (t:Track)-[:FOLLOWS]->(e:GeoEntity)\n// (e:GeoEntity)-[:CORRELATED_WITH {score, type, time}]->(t:Threat)\n// (e:GeoEntity)-[:ENTERED]->(f:GeoFence)\n// (t:Threat)-[:TARGETS]->(c:Country)\n// (i:IOC)-[:OBSERVED_IN]->(c:Country)\n// (e:GeoEntity)-[:ALERTED {severity, score, timestamp}]->(a:Alert)\n\n// ── Création des contraintes ──\nCREATE CONSTRAINT geo_entity_id IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:GeoEntity) REQUIRE e.id IS UNIQUE;\nCREATE CONSTRAINT cluster_id IF NOT EXISTS FOR (c:Cluster) REQUIRE c.id IS UNIQUE;\nCREATE CONSTRAINT threat_id IF NOT EXISTS FOR (t:Threat) REQUIRE t.id IS UNIQUE;\nCREATE CONSTRAINT alert_id IF NOT EXISTS FOR (a:Alert) REQUIRE a.id IS UNIQUE;\n\n// ── Index géospatiaux ──\nCREATE INDEX geo_entity_location IF NOT EXISTS FOR (e:GeoEntity) ON (e.lat, e.lon);\nCREATE INDEX threat_location IF NOT EXISTS FOR (t:Threat) ON (t.lat, t.lon);\nCREATE INDEX alert_timestamp IF NOT EXISTS FOR (a:Alert) ON (a.timestamp);\n```\n\n### 7.2 Requêtes d'Intégration\n\n```cypher\n// ── 1. Entités proches d'une menace active ──\nMATCH (e:GeoEntity)-[r:CORRELATED_WITH]->(t:Threat)\nWHERE t.severity IN ['CRITICAL', 'HIGH']\n  AND r.score > 70\n  AND t.timestamp > datetime() - duration('PT6H')\nRETURN e.id, e.type, e.label, t.title, r.score, r.type AS correlation_type\nORDER BY r.score DESC\nLIMIT 50\n\n// ── 2. Clusters à haute densité d'alertes ──\nMATCH (c:Cluster)<-[:LOCATED_IN]-(e:GeoEntity)\nWITH c, count(e) AS entity_count\nWHERE entity_count > 10\nMATCH (e:GeoEntity)-[:ALERTED]->(a:Alert)\nWHERE a.timestamp > datetime() - duration('PT1H')\nWITH c, entity_count, count(a) AS alert_count\nWHERE alert_count > 3\nRETURN c.id, c.center_lat, c.center_lon, entity_count, alert_count\nORDER BY alert_count DESC\n\n// ── 3. Chemins de corrélation (entité → cluster → menace → pays) ──\nMATCH path = (e:GeoEntity)-[:LOCATED_IN]->(c:Cluster)\n             (e)-[:CORRELATED_WITH]->(t:Threat)\n             (t)-[:TARGETS]->(country:Country)\nWHERE e.last_seen > datetime() - duration('PT1H')\nRETURN path\nLIMIT 20\n\n// ── 4. Entités ayant franchi une geofence récemment ──\nMATCH (e:GeoEntity)-[:ENTERED]->(f:GeoFence)\nWHERE e.last_seen > datetime() - duration('PT30M')\nRETURN e.id, e.type, e.label, f.name, f.type, f.severity\nORDER BY f.severity DESC\n\n// ── 5. Statistiques de corrélation par type ──\nMATCH (e:GeoEntity)-[r:CORRELATED_WITH]->(t:Threat)\nRETURN e.type AS entity_type, \n       t.type AS threat_type, \n       count(r) AS correlation_count,\n       avg(r.score) AS avg_score,\n       max(r.score) AS max_score\nORDER BY correlation_count DESC\n```\n\n### 7.3 Graph Writer Service\n\n```typescript\n// ── Service d'écriture vers Neo4j ──\nclass GeoThreatGraphWriter {\n  private driver: Driver;\n  private batch: GraphOperation[] = [];\n  private batchSize: number = 100;\n  private flushInterval: number = 1000; // 1s\n\n  constructor() {\n    this.driver = neo4j.driver(\n      process.env.NEO4J_URI || 'bolt://graph:7687',\n      neo4j.auth.basic(\n        process.env.NEO4J_USER || 'neo4j',\n        process.env.NEO4J_PASSWORD || 'password'\n      )\n    );\n    \n    // Flush périodique\n    setInterval(() => this.flush(), this.flushInterval);\n  }\n\n  async writeEntity(entity: TrackedEntity): Promise<void> {\n    this.batch.push({\n      statement: `\n        MERGE (e:GeoEntity {id: $id})\n        SET e.type = $type,\n            e.label = $label,\n            e.lat = $lat,\n            e.lon = $lng,\n            e.last_seen = datetime($lastSeen),\n            e.speed = $speed,\n            e.heading = $heading,\n            e.status = $status\n      `,\n      parameters: {\n        id: entity.id,\n        type: entity.type,\n        label: entity.label,\n        lat: entity.lat,\n        lng: entity.lng,\n        lastSeen: new Date(entity.timestamp).toISOString(),\n        speed: entity.speed || 0,\n        heading: entity.heading || 0,\n        status: 'ACTIVE',\n      },\n    });\n\n    if (this.batch.length >= this.batchSize) {\n      await this.flush();\n    }\n  }\n\n  async writeCorrelation(correlation: GeoThreatCorrelation): Promise<void> {\n    this.batch.push({\n      statement: `\n        MATCH (e:GeoEntity {id: $entityId})\n        MATCH (t:Threat {id: $threatId})\n        MERGE (e)-[r:CORRELATED_WITH {type: $relationType}]->(t)\n        SET r.score = $score,\n            r.severity = $severity,\n            r.distance = $distance,\n            r.time_diff_hours = $timeDiff,\n            r.timestamp = datetime($timestamp),\n            r.description = $description\n      `,\n      parameters: {\n        entityId: correlation.entityId,\n        threatId: correlation.threatId,\n        relationType: correlation.relationType,\n        score: correlation.score,\n        severity: correlation.severity,\n        distance: correlation.distance,\n        timeDiff: correlation.timeDiffHours,\n        timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n        description: correlation.description,\n      },\n    });\n  }\n\n  async writeAlert(alert: ProcessedAlert): Promise<void> {\n    this.batch.push({\n      statement: `\n        MATCH (e:GeoEntity {id: $entityId})\n        CREATE (a:Alert {\n          id: $alertId,\n          type: $type,\n          severity: $severity,\n          score: $score,\n          description: $description,\n          timestamp: datetime($timestamp),\n          expires_at: datetime($expiresAt)\n        })\n        MERGE (e)-[:ALERTED {severity: $severity, score: $score}]->(a)\n      `,\n      parameters: {\n        entityId: alert.entity_id,\n        alertId: alert.id,\n        type: alert.type,\n        severity: alert.severity,\n        score: alert.score,\n        description: alert.description,\n        timestamp: alert.timestamp,\n        expiresAt: alert.expires_at,\n      },\n    });\n  }\n\n  private async flush(): Promise<void> {\n    if (this.batch.length === 0) return;\n\n    const operations = [...this.batch];\n    this.batch = [];\n\n    const session = this.driver.session();\n    try {\n      const tx = session.beginTransaction();\n      for (const op of operations) {\n        await tx.run(op.statement, op.parameters);\n      }\n      await tx.commit();\n    } catch (error) {\n      console.error('[GraphWriter] Batch write failed:', error);\n      // Re-queue en cas d'erreur\n      this.batch.unshift(...operations);\n    } finally {\n      await session.close();\n    }\n  }\n}\n\ninterface GraphOperation {\n  statement: string;\n  parameters: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. API du Module\n\n### 8.1 Endpoints REST\n\n```\nPOST   /api/geo/ingest                    # Ingérer une position d'entité\nGET    /api/geo/entity/:id                # Détails d'une entité\nGET    /api/geo/entity/:id/trajectory     # Trajectoire d'une entité\nGET    /api/geo/entity/:id/anomalies      # Anomalies d'une entité\nGET    /api/geo/clusters                  # Clusters géospatiaux\nPOST   /api/geo/correlate                 # Corrélation géo-menace\nGET    /api/geo/correlations              # Corrélations actives\nGET    /api/geo/fences                    # Zones géographiques\nPOST   /api/geo/fences                    # Créer une zone\nGET    /api/geo/nearby                    # Entités proches d'un point\nGET    /api/threat/active                 # Menaces actives\nGET    /api/threat/:id                    # Détails d'une menace\nGET    /api/threat/cve/:id                # Détails CVE\nPOST   /api/threat/ingest                 # Ingérer une menace (API externe)\nGET    /api/alert/recent                  # Alertes récentes\nGET    /api/alert/:id                     # Détails d'une alerte\nPOST   /api/alert/ack                     # Acquitter une alerte\nGET    /api/stats/geo                     # Statistiques géospatiales\nGET    /api/stats/threat                  # Statistiques menaces\n```\n\n### 8.2 WebSocket Events\n\n```\nClient → Serveur:\n├── subscribe:geo:positions      → Recevoir les positions en temps réel\n├── subscribe:geo:anomalies      → Recevoir les anomalies détectées\n├── subscribe:geo:alerts         → Recevoir les alertes\n├── subscribe:geo:clusters       → Recevoir les clusters mis à jour\n├── subscribe:threat:updates     → Recevoir les mises à jour de menaces\n├── geo:query:nearby {lat, lng, radius}  → Requête ponctuelle\n└── geo:track {entity_id}        → Suivre une entité spécifique\n\nServeur → Client:\n├── geo:position {entity_id, lat, lng, type, speed, heading}\n├── geo:anomaly {entity_id, type, severity, description}\n├── geo:alert {id, severity, type, description}\n├── geo:cluster {id, center, density, radius}\n├── threat:update {id, type, severity, location}\n├── correlation:new {entity_id, threat_id, score, type}\n└── heartbeat {timestamp}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 9. Dashboard Carte Interactive\n\n### 9.1 Composants de la Carte\n\n```\n┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                      OSIRIS MAP COMPONENT                                   │\n│                                                                              │\n│  <MapCanvas>                                                                │\n│  ├── <BaseMap />                    # Fond de carte (MapLibre GL)           │\n│  │   ├── dark basemap              # Mode nuit                             │\n│  │   └── satellite basemap         # Imagerie satellite                    │\n│  │                                                                          │\n│  ├── <GeoEntityLayers />            # Couches d'entités                    │\n│  │   ├── <FlightLayer />            # ADS-B avec trajectoires              │\n│  │   │   ├── Points animés (position actuelle)                             │\n│  │   │   ├── Trajectoires (ligne de vol)                                   │\n│  │   │   └── Anomalies (icône warning si loitering/descente rapide)        │\n│  │   │                                                                      │\n│  │   ├── <MaritimeLayer />          # AIS avec routes                     │\n│  │   │   ├── Navires (icônes par type: cargo, tanker, etc.)               │\n│  │   │   ├── Routes prévues (lignes pointillées)                          │\n│  │   │   └── Ports + chokepoints (marqueurs)                              │\n│  │   │                                                                      │\n│  │   ├── <SatelliteLayer />         # Satellites + orbites                │\n│  │   │   ├── Positions (points animés)                                     │\n│  │   │   ├── Orbites (lignes orbitales)                                    │\n│  │   │   └── Fenêtres de passage (overlay)                                │\n│  │   │                                                                      │\n│  │   ├── <CCTVLayer />              # Caméras publiques                   │\n│  │   │   └── Marqueurs avec vignette live                                 │\n│  │   │                                                                      │\n│  │   └── <InfrastructureLayer />    # Infrastructure critique              │\n│  │       ├── Câbles sous-marins (lignes)                                   │\n│  │       ├── Centrales nucléaires (marqueurs)                             │\n│  │       └── Pipelines (lignes)                                           │\n│  │                                                                          │\n│  ├── <ThreatLayers />               # Couches de menaces                  │\n│  │   ├── <ConflictZoneLayer />      # Zones de conflit (polygones)        │\n│  │   │   ├── Polygones de conflit (rouge translucide)                     │\n│  │   │   ├── Frontlines (lignes)                                          │\n│  │   │   └── Sanctions (hachuré)                                          │\n│  │   │                                                                      │\n│  │   ├── <CyberThreatLayer />       # IOC géolocalisés                   │\n│  │   │   ├── Malware C2 (points rouges)                                   │\n│  │   │   └── Heatmap densité (dégradé)                                    │\n│  │   │                                                                      │\n│  │   └── <NaturalEventLayer />      # Événements naturels                 │\n│  │       ├── Séismes (cercles proportionnels magnitude)                   │\n│  │       ├── Incendies (points orange)                                    │\n│  │       └── Météo (polygones alertes)                                    │\n│  │                                                                          │\n│  ├── <IntelLayers />                # Couches intelligence                │\n│  │   ├── <H3HeatmapLayer />         # Carte de chaleur H3                 │\n│  │   ├── <ClusterLayer />           # Clusters d'entités                  │\n│  │   ├── <CorrelationLayer />       # Lignes de corrélation              │\n│  │   └── <AlertLayer />             # Alertes actives (pulsations)        │\n│  │                                                                          │\n│  ├── <InteractionHandlers />        # Gestion des interactions            │\n│  │   ├── onClick → EntityPopup (détails)                                  │\n│  │   ├── onRightClick → RegionDossier (IA)                                │\n│  │   ├── onHover → Tooltip (infos rapides)                               │\n│  │   ├── onDrag → Mise à jour viewport                                    │\n│  │   └── onZoom → Clustering adaptatif                                    │\n│  │                                                                          │\n│  ├── <Controls />                    # Contrôles de la carte              │\n│  │   ├── Zoom +/-                                                        │\n│  │   ├── Projection (Globe / Mercator)                                    │\n│  │   ├── Style (Dark / Satellite)                                         │\n│  │   ├── Geocoder (recherche adresse)                                    │\n│  │   └── Fullscreen                                                       │\n│  │                                                                          │\n│  └── <Overlays />                    # Superpositions                     │\n│      ├── ScaleBar (échelle)                                               │\n│      ├── MouseCoords (lat, lng)                                          │\n│      ├── DayNightTerminator (ligne jour/nuit)                             │\n│      └── Legend (légende des couches)                                     │\n└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘\n```\n\n### 9.2 Rendus des Anomalies sur la Carte\n\n```typescript\n// ── Rendu des anomalies sur la carte MapLibre ──\nclass AnomalyLayerRenderer {\n  private map: maplibregl.Map;\n  private anomalySource: maplibregl.GeoJSONSource;\n\n  constructor(map: maplibregl.Map) {\n    this.map = map;\n    this.initializeLayer();\n  }\n\n  private initializeLayer() {\n    // Source pour les anomalies\n    this.map.addSource('anomalies', {\n      type: 'geojson',\n      data: { type: 'FeatureCollection', features: [] },\n    });\n\n    // Couche des anomalies (points avec pulsation)\n    this.map.addLayer({\n      id: 'anomalies-heat',\n      type: 'circle',\n      source: 'anomalies',\n      paint: {\n        'circle-radius': [\n          'case',\n          ['==', ['get', 'severity'], 'CRITICAL'], 24,\n          ['==', ['get', 'severity'], 'HIGH'], 18,\n          ['==', ['get', 'severity'], 'MEDIUM'], 12,\n          8\n        ],\n        'circle-color': [\n          'case',\n          ['==', ['get', 'severity'], 'CRITICAL'], '#FF3D3D',\n          ['==', ['get', 'severity'], 'HIGH'], '#FF9500',\n          ['==', ['get', 'severity'], 'MEDIUM'], '#FFD600',\n          '#4CAF50'\n        ],\n        'circle-opacity': 0.7,\n        'circle-blur': 0.5,\n      },\n    });\n\n    // Icônes de pulsation pour CRITICAL\n    this.map.addLayer({\n      id: 'anomalies-pulse',\n      type: 'circle',\n      source: 'anomalies',\n      filter: ['==', ['get', 'severity'], 'CRITICAL'],\n      paint: {\n        'circle-radius': 32,\n        'circle-color': '#FF3D3D',\n        'circle-opacity': 0.2,\n        'circle-blur': 1,\n      },\n    });\n\n    // Étiquettes des anomalies\n    this.map.addLayer({\n      id: 'anomalies-label',\n      type: 'symbol',\n      source: 'anomalies',\n      layout: {\n        'text-field': ['get', 'type'],\n        'text-size': 10,\n        'text-offset': [0, 1.5],\n        'text-anchor': 'top',\n      },\n      paint: {\n        'text-color': '#ffffff',\n        'text-halo-color': '#000000',\n        'text-halo-width': 1,\n      },\n    });\n  }\n\n  updateAnomalies(anomalies: Anomaly[]) {\n    const features = anomalies.map(a => ({\n      type: 'Feature' as const,\n      geometry: {\n        type: 'Point' as const,\n        coordinates: [a.position.lng, a.position.lat],\n      },\n      properties: {\n        id: a.entity_id,\n        type: a.type,\n        severity: a.severity,\n        description: a.description,\n        score: a.score,\n        confidence: a.confidence,\n        timestamp: a.timestamp,\n      },\n    }));\n\n    const source = this.map.getSource('anomalies') as maplibregl.GeoJSONSource;\n    source.setData({\n      type: 'FeatureCollection',\n      features,\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n// ── Rendu des corrélations (lignes) ──\nclass CorrelationLayerRenderer {\n  private map: maplibregl.Map;\n\n  constructor(map: maplibregl.Map) {\n    this.map = map;\n    this.initializeLayer();\n  }\n\n  private initializeLayer() {\n    this.map.addSource('correlations', {\n      type: 'geojson',\n      data: { type: 'FeatureCollection', features: [] },\n    });\n\n    this.map.addLayer({\n      id: 'correlations-line',\n      type: 'line',\n      source: 'correlations',\n      paint: {\n        'line-color': [\n          'case',\n          ['==', ['get', 'severity'], 'CRITICAL'], '#FF3D3D',\n          ['==', ['get', 'severity'], 'HIGH'], '#FF9500',\n          '#D4AF37'\n        ],\n        'line-width': [\n          'case',\n          ['>=', ['get', 'score'], 85], 3,\n          ['>=', ['get', 'score'], 70], 2,\n          1\n        ],\n        'line-opacity': 0.6,\n        'line-dasharray': [3, 3],\n      },\n    });\n\n    // Animation des lignes\n    this.map.on('render', () => {\n      if (this.map.getLayer('correlations-line')) {\n        this.map.setPaintProperty('correlations-line', 'line-dasharray', [\n          Math.sin(Date.now() / 1000) * 2 + 3,\n          3\n        ]);\n      }\n    });\n  }\n\n  updateCorrelations(correlations: GeoThreatCorrelation[]) {\n    const features = correlations.map(c => ({\n      type: 'Feature' as const,\n      geometry: {\n        type: 'LineString' as const,\n        coordinates: [\n          [c.entityPosition.lng, c.entityPosition.lat],\n          [c.threatPosition.lng, c.threatPosition.lat],\n        ],\n      },\n      properties: {\n        id: c.id,\n        score: c.score,\n        severity: c.severity,\n        type: c.relationType,\n        description: c.description,\n      },\n    }));\n\n    const source = this.map.getSource('correlations') as maplibregl.GeoJSONSource;\n    source.setData({\n      type: 'FeatureCollection',\n      features,\n    });\n  }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 10. Déploiement & Scaling\n\n### 10.1 Docker Compose\n\n```yaml\n# docker-compose.geo-threat.yml\nversion: \"3.9\"\n\nservices:\n  # GEOINT Engine\n  geoint:\n    build: ./modules/geoint\n    container_name: osiris-geoint\n    ports:\n      - \"5002:5002\"\n    environment:\n      REDIS_URL: redis://redis:6379\n      NEO4J_URI: bolt://graph:7687\n      H3_RESOLUTION: 8\n      ANOMALY_THRESHOLD: \"2.5\"\n    volumes:\n      - geoint-data:/data\n    depends_on:\n      - redis\n      - graph\n    networks:\n      - osiris-net\n\n  # Threat Engine  \n  threat:\n    build: ./modules/threat\n    container_name: osiris-threat\n    ports:\n      - \"5003:5003\"\n    environment:\n      REDIS_URL: redis://redis:6379\n      CISA_API_KEY: ${CISA_API_KEY}\n      SHODAN_API_KEY: ${SHODAN_API_KEY}\n      ABUSECH_API_KEY: ${ABUSECH_API_KEY}\n    volumes:\n      - threat-data:/data\n    depends_on:\n      - redis\n    networks:\n      - osiris-net\n\n  # Fusion Service\n  fusion:\n    build: ./modules/fusion\n    container_name: osiris-fusion\n    ports:\n      - \"5006:5006\"\n    environment:\n      REDIS_URL: redis://redis:6379\n      NEO4J_URI: bolt://graph:7687\n      ALERT_COOLDOWN: \"300\"\n    depends_on:\n      - redis\n      - graph\n      - geoint\n      - threat\n    networks:\n      - osiris-net\n\n  # Tile Server (Vector tiles temps réel)\n  tiles:\n    image: maptiler/tileserver-gl\n    container_name: osiris-tiles\n    ports:\n      - \"5007:8080\"\n    volumes:\n      - ./data/tiles:/data\n    networks:\n      - osiris-net\n\n  # Graph Database\n  graph:\n    image: neo4j:5\n    container_name: osiris-graph\n    ports:\n      - \"7687:7687\"  # Bolt\n      - \"7474:7474\"  # HTTP\n    environment:\n      NEO4J_AUTH: neo4j/${NEO4J_PASSWORD:-password}\n      NEO4J_PLUGINS: '[\"apoc\", \"graph-data-science\"]'\n    volumes:\n      - neo4j-data:/data\n      - neo4j-logs:/logs\n    networks:\n      - osiris-net\n\n  # Search Engine\n  search:\n    image: getmeili/meilisearch:v1.3\n    container_name: osiris-search\n    ports:\n      - \"5005:7700\"\n    environment:\n      MEILI_MASTER_KEY: ${MEILI_MASTER_KEY:-masterKey}\n    volumes:\n      - meili-data:/meili_data\n    networks:\n      - osiris-net\n\nvolumes:\n  geoint-data:\n  threat-data:\n  neo4j-data:\n  neo4j-logs:\n  meili-data:\n\nnetworks:\n  osiris-net:\n    driver: bridge\n```\n\n### 10.2 Performance & Scaling\n\n```\nEstimation Charge:\n├── Positions entités: 50K/min (aviation + maritime + satellites)\n├── Événements menace: 1K/min (CVE + IOC + GDELT)\n├── Corrélations: 10K/min (géospatiales)\n├── Alertes: 10/min (CRITICAL + HIGH)\n└── Requêtes API: 100/s\n\nScaling:\n├── GEOINT Engine\n│   ├── 1 instance pour ingestion + analyse\n│   ├── +1 instance par 100K positions/min\n│   └── Sharding par type d'entité (aviation, maritime, satellites)\n│\n├── Threat Engine\n│   ├── 1 instance pour ingestion + scoring\n│   ├── +1 instance par source majeure\n│   └── Cache Redis des résultats de scoring (TTL 5 min)\n│\n├── Fusion Service\n│   ├── 1 instance (IO-bound, Redis streams)\n│   └── +1 instance si > 100 alertes/min\n│\n├── Neo4j\n│   ├── Primary + 1 replica pour HA\n│   ├── 8GB RAM recommandé\n│   └── Index géospatiaux optimisés\n│\n└── Redis\n    ├── Cluster 3 nœuds\n    ├── 4GB RAM par nœud\n    └── Persistence AOF every 1s\n```\n\n### 10.3 Métriques Clés\n\n| Métrique | Description | Seuil Alerte |\n|----------|-------------|-------------|\n| `geo_entities_tracked` | Entités actives | - |\n| `geo_anomalies_detected` | Anomalies/minute | > 10/min |\n| `geo_anomaly_accuracy` | Précision anomalies | < 70% |\n| `geo_correlations_generated` | Corrélations/minute | - |\n| `geo_correlation_score_avg` | Score moyen | < 50 |\n| `threat_events_ingested` | Menaces/heure | - |\n| `threat_critical_count` | Menaces CRITICAL actives | > 5 |\n| `alert_generated_total` | Alertes totales | - |\n| `alert_response_time_ms` | Temps de traitement alerte | > 500ms |\n| `geo_pipeline_latency_ms` | Latence pipeline | > 1000ms |\n| `ws_connections_active` | Connexions WebSocket | - |\n| `neo4j_query_time_p50` | Temps requête Neo4j | > 200ms |\n\n---\n\n## Annexe : Scénarios d'Usage\n\n### Scénario 1 : Détection de loitering naval près d'un chokepoint\n\n```\n1. AIS reçoit position d'un cargo dans le Détroit d'Ormuz\n2. TrajectoryAnalyzer détecte loitering (vitesse < 1 km/h depuis 30 min)\n3. GeoFenceEngine confirme: dans zone chokepoint STRAT_OF_HORMUZ\n4. Anomaly Detector: LOITERING (HIGH) + confiance 85%\n5. GeoThreatCorrelator: corrèle avec menaces actives (pirates, sanctions Iran)\n6. ThreatScorer: score = 78 (HIGH) - cargo près de zone à risque\n7. AlertFusionEngine: alerte HIGH, dispatch WebSocket + dashboard\n8. GraphEngine: écrit noeud GeoEntity, relation CORRELATED_WITH, noeud Alert\n9. Frontend: affiche cargo avec pulsation orange, ligne de corrélation rouge\n10. Analyste: reçoit recommandations, peut expand le graphe pour contexte\n```\n\n### Scénario 2 : Vol militaire près d'une zone de conflit\n\n```\n1. ADS-B reçoit position d'un avion militaire (catégorie militaire)\n2. TrajectoryAnalyzer: trajectoire anormale (cap changeant, vitesse irrégulière)\n3. GeoFenceEngine: avion s'approche de UKRAINE_CONFLICT_ZONE (< 50 km)\n4. Anomaly Detector: SHARP_TURN (plusieurs virages > 120°)\n5. GeoThreatCorrelator: corrélation avec conflit Ukraine (CRITICAL)\n6. ThreatScorer: score = 92 (CRITICAL) - avion militaire près zone conflit\n7. AlertFusionEngine: alerte CRITICAL, dispatch WebSocket + notification\n8. GraphEngine: écrit relations, met à jour cluster\n9. Frontend: affiche trajectoire rouge, alerte pulsante, popup avec scoring\n10. Analyste: vérifie squawk code, NOTAMs, tendances régionales\n```\n\n---\n\n*Document du module GEOINT + Threat Intelligence généré pour Osiris-Lab v4.2 — 18 juin 2026*","operationIsLocatedInWorkspace":true,"startLineNumbers":[]}

### Architecture du Module

```
Sources Géospatiales          Threat Intelligence         Fusion
═══════════════════          ════════════════════        ══════

AIS (Maritime)  ───┤        abuse.ch ──────────┤

CCTV Cameras    ───┤  Engine │  VirusTotal ────────┤  Engine  │  Service
Earthquakes     ───┤  (:5002)│  AlienVault OTX ────┤  (:5003) │  (:5006)
...             ───┘        Sanctions ─────────────┘          │
       │                                      │               │

                      │                                       │
                      ▼                                       ▼
       ┌──────────────────────────────┐    ┌──────────────────┐

       └──────────────────────────────┘    └──────────────────┘
```

### Détails Techniques

| Section | Contenu |
|---------|---------|
| **1. Architecture du Module** | Vue d'ensemble, flux intégré, 5 services (ports 5002-5008) |
| **2. GEOINT Engine** | 4 couches (ingestion, analyse spatiale H3, détection anomalie, publication), H3 hexagonal indexing (résolution 6-10), TrajectoryAnalyzer avec rolling window 60 min, GeoFenceEngine avec algorithm Ray-Casting |
| **3. Threat Intelligence Engine** | 4 couches (ingestion, enrichissement, corrélation géo-menace, scoring), GeoThreatCorrelator avec seuils spatiaux (50-500km) et temporels (6-72h), ThreatScorer composite (5 facteurs pondérés) |
| **4. Pipeline de Données** | Pipeline 7 stages complet avec FastAPI service, 5 endpoints REST + WebSocket, Redis streams |
| **5. Détection d'Anomalies** | 5 types (LOITERING, SHARP_TURN, SPEED_ANOMALY, RAPID_DESCENT, GEO_FENCE), 4 modèles (statistique Z-score, règles expertes, ML, corrélation inter-anomalies) |
| **6. Système d'Alertes** | AlertFusionEngine (déduplication 5 min, cooldown adaptatif, enrichissement contexte, priorité 1-100), AlertDispatcher (4 canaux: WebSocket, notification, webhook, dashboard) |
| **7. Intégration Graphe Global** | Schéma Neo4j (4 nœuds GEOINT, 4 nœuds Threat, 7 relations, 3 contraintes, 3 index), 5 requêtes d'intégration, GraphWriter service batch |
| **8. API du Module** | 19 endpoints REST documentés, 13 événements WebSocket bidirectionnels |
| **9. Dashboard Carte Interactive** | Arbre de composants MapLibre GL complet (25+ couches), AnomalyLayerRenderer avec pulsations CRITICAL, CorrelationLayerRenderer avec lignes animées |
| **10. Déploiement & Scaling** | Docker Compose 6 services, estimation charge (50K positions/min, 1K menaces/min, 10K corrélations/min), 12 métriques clés avec seuils d'alerte |
